# Home Hub v1.3 — Homework: plans, focus sessions and points (design)

This is the build contract for v1.3, "Homework". It is written against the hub as it will be when v1.3 ships: schema version 4, that is `0002_home_hub_v2.sql`, `0003_google_calendar.sql` and `0004_meals_and_shopping.sql`, the `hub-jobs` Edge Function and `supabase/cron.sql`. The live database is at schema 3 today; 0004 (v1.2) is merged and is applied before 0005. Line references are to the repository at `aced5bb` (`2de3b44` has the same files). Every table, column and function named here either exists there or is added by migration `0005_homework.sql` (§3), which refuses to run without 0004, or by the next migrations, `0006_homework_school.sql` (§12) and `0007_homework_coach.sql`, the coach view (§20). The timelines (§21) are pages only, with no migration. v1.3 is built as two PRs on the one migration: **PR A** (everything but the AI's Edge Function part) and **PR B** (the AI planner), §2. It was put together from three independent drafts (a smallest-first one, a kid-first one and a trust-first one) and four research notes; §18 says what came from where and what was left out, and §19 what three adversarial reviews changed.

What the owner asked for: "Lesson planning and homework for Jordan. When he has a 2-week assignment he needs help breaking it into milestones and benchmarks — executive-functioning help; […] Needs rewards, timers etc." (One clause about Jordan is left out on purpose: this repository holds no label about him.) The owner then answered four questions; their answers are decided (§1.1):
1. Assignments come from the school's **Blackbaud** system.
2. Breaking a task into chunks: **AI assistance, with the tutor (Pat Lee) helping** Jordan.
3. Rewards: **points** for starting on time, finishing focus sessions and hitting milestones; Jordan trades them for **rewards the parents list**; **a parent approves each trade**.
4. "Lesson planning" means **all of**: his homework time (when he works on what), lessons a parent or the tutor plans for him, and his class and activity schedule (homework time fits around them).

And timers: focus sessions.

Six rules follow:
- **Help where the work happens.** The next step, the timer, the stuck sheet and the points are on the screen Jordan has open while he works: his My day link. A planning page alone doesn't carry over to Tuesday at 4:30.
- **Jordan owns the plan; grown-ups coach.** He writes down his own homework, picks the session length, his start cue and his if-stuck plan, moves and re-plans plain steps and asks for rewards. Pat and the parents plan big work with him, check milestones with a word about the work, and approve trades. Nobody gets a minute-by-minute feed, and nothing shows anyone whether he is working right now.
- **Pat helps without seeing the family, and the parents see what Pat tells him.** Pat gets a link that shows Jordan's homework and nothing else. Pat never signs in, is never a person on the family list, and never gets Jordan's My day link. Every answer and note Pat writes to Jordan stays visible to the parents.
- **The AI suggests; people decide.** Pat or a parent asks for a draft; a person changes it with Jordan and saves it. Jordan's own link never reaches the AI. The AI never does the work.
- **Same output when unused.** With homework not turned on for anyone, every email, text, My day answer and wall event comes out byte for byte as on schema 4 (§3.7).
- **No labels.** This is executive-function support that suits any kid: starting, timing and planning. Nothing in the repository gives Jordan a medical or learning label, and nothing asks for or stores one (§13.1 item 8).

People. Examples use the realdb household (`tests/realdb/seed_v2.sql`), as v1.1 and v1.2 do:
- Alex and Morgan are the parents (admins).
- Sam is an adult who signs in.
- Jordan is a kid, about 12 to 14.
- Taylor is the helper, and reads Spanish.
- **Pat Lee is Jordan's tutor.** Pat is not on the family list and is never a `people` row: "Pat Lee, tutor" is only the label of a tutor link (§3.2). Like every name in this repository, it is made up.

The database tests' household (`tests/db/fixtures.sql`) differs: there Alex is the admin, Sam the adult, Jordan a parent who was removed, Taylor the kid and Morgan the Spanish-speaking helper. §14 names people as each suite has them. "Pat Lee" is also the made-up electrician contractor in the fixtures; §14 says how the two are kept apart.

The page is called **Homework**. Jordan's part of My day is "Homework" too ("Tarea" in Spanish).

## 0. Decisions on one page

- **One migration, two PRs, then a second release.**
  - **PR A, v1.3 "Homework"** (`0005_homework.sql`, schema 5): typed assignments and lessons, and homework Jordan writes down himself; plans of steps and milestones; starter plans; homework time with Jordan's busy time taken out; Jordan's homework screen, focus timer, stuck sheet and **Re-plan** on his My day; Pat's link; points and rewards, with Jordan asking and a parent approving each trade. The migration also holds the AI's tables and SQL functions, unused until PR B. No Edge Function change, so v1.2's deploy order: the site, then the paste.
  - **PR B, v1.3 "AI planner"** (no migration): the `homework_ai` action in `hub-jobs`, and the AI parts of the pages (**Send to Claude**, the preview, drafts, **Test**, the switch). Every unsettled point (Anthropic's API, the background task, the owner's Anthropic account) is in this PR, so a hold-up there can't hold back Jordan's screen. If it needs an SQL change after all, that is a small migration of its own.
  - **0006** (the next release, schema 6): the school feed: the school's Blackbaud calendar links, copied on a parent's login (his assignments, and his class schedule and games as busy time), kept as secrets and read hourly by `hub-jobs`; "New from school" on his screen; the parents' status card. One more `cron.sql` job, no new secret. §11, §12. Still to come, each additive: the homework line in his text and the parents' email; Jordan drafting his own plan, with an AI "What's missing?" that Pat runs; his pace applied when steps are placed (§12.6).
- **Homework is its own data.** Assignments, steps, focus sessions and points are new tables, not tasks or events. They stay out of `agenda`, Late, "Nobody's on it", the Tasks page, the Calendar page, the email, the texts and the wall, as v1.2 kept dinners out ("A dinner is not an event", `docs/v1.2-kitchen.md:461`).
- **Homework time is an ordinary repeating event** ("Homework", Mon–Thu 4:30–5:15, Jordan in charge), linked to Jordan in a small table. Days off, one-day changes and cancellations work as for any event, on the Calendar page. Steps are placed only into that time, **minus Jordan's busy time** (his other timed events and his Google events), so homework time fits around classes and activities from the first release. Placement looks at most 8 weeks ahead.
- **Jordan's homework is on his My day.** A card with the next step and its own **Start**; behind it **Now** (the next step, its "done looks like" line, his own start cue, one big **Start**), **Tonight**, **Due soon** (work left against homework time left), and his points. The timer is a shrinking ring that counts from the server's clock. He writes down his own homework (**+ Homework**), and **Quick** makes a small one tonight's step in one tap. Behind on a plan, **Re-plan** moves his plain steps from today; milestones are moved with Pat.
- **Getting unstuck is a sheet, not a lecture:** his own if-then line, **Make it smaller** (a smaller step he types; the running block moves to it), **Ask Pat** (a short question Pat answers on the link), **Next step**. No AI on his link.
- **Pat Lee gets a tutor link** (`day.html?h=<token>`): Jordan's assignments, plans, questions and milestones to check, his homework time with "Busy" blocks (no titles), his finished sessions and notes, his pace. Never the family's calendar, list, dinners, people, phone numbers, points or rewards, and nothing live: not whether he is working now, nor whether he is alone. The link has an end date (180 days, **Extend**), a "last used" time, **New link** and **Off**.
- **The AI drafts plans and nothing else** (PR B). A new `hub-jobs` action, `homework_ai`, calls **Claude Opus 5.5** (`claude-opus-5-5`, effort `medium`, structured output) over HTTPS with the function's own `fetch`, with no SDK. Pat's link or a parent asks; Jordan's link can't. It is **off until a parent has run Test, listed the words never to send (the school's name, teachers' names) and turned it on**. The asker sees **What Claude will see** whenever what would be sent has changed. The answer is a draft labelled "Suggested by AI (Claude)"; it becomes the plan only when a person saves it. Every failure leaves a starter plan. About 6 cents a plan; caps in the database (12 a day, 150 a month) and a $15 monthly limit at Anthropic.
- **No photos in v1.3.** The teacher's text is pasted; the page says how to copy it from a photo with the phone's Live Text, which sends nothing anywhere.
- **Points, earn-only, for what he controls, by the server's clock** (owner decision 2 sets the numbers): 1 point per minute the server saw of a block on his homework, **paid the second the block ends**, whatever he answers then (done, more to do, or stop), with a small celebration on his screen (the counter ticks up, "+N", a short sound if sound is on); while it runs, the timer counts the points up live, never past what the server will pay. +10 for starting the day's first block on time, paid once 5 minutes of it are seen; +40 for a milestone, **+20 when he ticks it and +20 when Pat or a parent checks it**. At most 60 a day from blocks and on-time starts, and 160 a week from milestones. Nothing is ever taken away as a penalty: **Not yet** keeps the first half, and an uncheck takes back only the check's half, never below zero; no streaks; no points for grades, for making steps, or for his own projects. A **next-reward bar** is always on his homework screens: "6 points to A new book", or "You can get … now!".
- **Trades:** Jordan asks from his phone, the points are held, and a parent taps **Yes** or **Not now** on Today or on the Homework page, with his balance and where this week's points came from beside it. **Instant rewards** (small ones the parents mark instant) he gets on the spot: the points go at once, at most 2 a day (the parents set it), from his free points only, and the parents see each on Today and on the Homework page.
- **Who sees it:** every grown-up who signs in reads it, and his screen says so; the parents act (Sam reads only). Taylor's link shows none of it. Pat never sees points, unless the parents turn on the link's coach view (0007, below).
- **0007, the coach view** (schema 7, §20): Pat is also Jordan's executive-function coach. A per-link switch, **off by default** and set only by the parents, lets the link also show, read-only, his points and rewards, his focus sessions, his own calendar and his daily tasks, and never the rest of the family; and lets Pat leave the parents a note, shown on the Homework page. His screen then says Pat sees his points too. No Edge Function and no `cron.sql` change.
- **Nothing new in the email, the texts or on the wall in v1.3.** The homework-time event shows wherever events show. Parents get one line on Today when an ask, a question or a milestone waits. Jordan's reminder to start is a repeating alarm on his phone, and a scheduled Focus keeps other apps quiet during homework time (README).
- **Nothing is kept longer than it needs to be:** drafts nobody used 30 days, AI run records 90 days, the teacher's text 30 days after the due date, done work, sessions, notes and questions 180 days. **Erase Jordan's homework** clears it all.
- **The owner's time and money** (§1.4): 3 minutes now (§1.3); PR A about 25 minutes (plus 5 if 0004 isn't applied yet); PR B about 15 minutes, optional; 0006 about 15 minutes. About **$3–4 a month** for the AI, never more than $15; nothing else costs more.

## 1. The owner's answers, and decisions for the owner

### 1.1 Decided (the owner's answers)

1. **Assignments come from Blackbaud.** The route a family can use is his **Assignments calendar feed**, which a parent's own login offers (confirmed, §11.1). It ships in 0006. Typed assignments work from v1.3 on, and stay; Jordan types his own too.
2. **Chunking: AI assistance, with the tutor helping.** The AI drafts; Pat (on the link) or a parent asks for it, edits it with Jordan and gives it to him. Earlier the owner had picked "Jordan, guided, and a parent checks"; the owner changed it to AI with the tutor helping. Jordan editing and drafting his own plans comes in step by step (plain steps and Re-plan in v1.3, whole drafts in 0006), so the AI's part shrinks as he gets better at it.
3. **Points for rewards the parents set,** for starting on time, finishing focus sessions and hitting milestones; **a parent approves each trade** (§8), except the small rewards the parents mark instant, which he gets on the spot (the owner added them later: §1.2 decision 15).
4. **"Lesson planning" is all three:** his homework time, lessons a parent or Pat plans, and his class and activity schedule, with homework time fitting around it (§5).
5. **Timers:** focus sessions of 10, 15, 20 or 25 minutes (§7).

### 1.2 Decisions with defaults

Each has a default, and the build goes ahead on it. Each can be changed later; **hard to undo** marks the ones that can't easily be.

1. **Who in the family sees homework.** Default: every grown-up who signs in reads it; only the parents change it. Sam reads; Taylor sees nothing. Jordan's screen says who sees what. (Parents only needs a change to the hub's core: §17.1.)
2. **Point values.** **The owner set them** ("immediate gratification", after the first build): 1 per focused minute, paid the second a block ends; +10 for an on-time start, paid once 5 minutes are seen; +40 a milestone, +20 when he ticks it and +20 when it's checked; at most 60 a day from focus and on-time starts, and 160 a week from milestones. About 340 to 380 points in two weeks (§8.1). The parents can change each value. **Hard to undo:** lowering points later feels like a penalty to him; the first build started lower (1 per 5 minutes, +5, +20, caps 20 and 80) and the owner raised them before he saw any. Tell him at the start that the family will look at the points with him after a term; a change agreed then isn't a penalty.
3. **Rewards.** Default: the parents write three to start: an **instant** one he can get most homework days (about 10 to 15 points), one about once a week (about 200), one bigger (about 500). He can suggest more; a parent prices them.
4. **Jordan writes down his own homework.** Default: yes (title, class, due date, his guess of the minutes); it earns like any homework, and the adults see "added by Jordan".
5. **Pat's link.** Default: a link with no sign-in that shows only Jordan's homework and ends after 180 days (**Extend**). Pat opens it on Pat's own phone or computer, not Jordan's.
6. **What Pat sees.** Default: his assignments with the teacher's text, plans, his questions, milestones to check, his homework time with "Busy" blocks, his finished sessions and notes. Not points, rewards, family names or events, and nothing live. With the coach view on (decision 16, §20), also his points and rewards, focus sessions, his own calendar and his daily tasks.
7. **Turning the AI on.** Default: off until a parent has run **Test**, listed the words never to send (the school's name, teachers' names) and turned it on; then Pat and the parents can ask, Jordan never. **Hard to undo:** what was sent can't be called back (Anthropic doesn't train on it; it may keep it up to 30 days, or up to 2 years if its safety checks flag it).
8. **AI model and money.** Default: Claude Opus 5.5 (about 6 cents a plan, $3–4 a month), a $15 monthly limit at Anthropic, and Anthropic's fallback when its safety check wrongly declines a request (re-run once on Claude Opus 5 or Opus 4.8). Cheaper: Claude Sonnet 5.5 (about half) or Claude Haiku 5.5 (about a fortieth), a one-line change after a comparison.
9. **The school's and Anthropic's rules on AI help.** Default: go ahead. The planner writes no schoolwork, Jordan never uses it himself, every draft is labelled and a person saves every plan. Pat or the owner asks the school once whether planning help is fine. Whether Anthropic's extra rules for products minors use apply to a family app is the owner's call (not legal advice).
10. **Photos of assignment sheets.** Default: none; the teacher's text is pasted (the phone's Live Text copies it from a photo and sends nothing).
11. **Focus lengths.** Default: 10, 15, 20 or 25 minutes, Jordan picks each time, **10 first** (the owner's choice: shorter blocks to start; the parents change it on the switches card); a 5-minute break suggested, not enforced.
12. **Homework time is a repeating event in his name.** Default: yes, so it shows on his My day, the Calendar page, his texts, the parents' email and the wall. Tick **Private** on it if the wall shouldn't name it.
13. **How long things are kept.** Default: the teacher's text 30 days after the due date; unused AI drafts 30 days; AI run records 90 days; done assignments, sessions, notes and questions 180 days; points until Erase.
14. **(0006) The school links.** Default: his Assignments link, checked once by a parent (and, if the parents like, his Schedule and Games/Practices links, as busy time). **Hard to undo:** the link works like a password and no way to cancel it was found; the hub never shows it again, never logs it, and **Remove** deletes it.
15. **Instant rewards.** The owner's decision, after the first build. A reward the parents mark **instant** he cashes on the spot from his phone: no ask and no parent's Yes; the points go at once; it must cost no more than his free points; at most a set number a day (`instant_day`, 2 by default, 0 to 10, per kid); the parents see each one (a Today line, the Homework page's Recent). The others keep the ask and the parent's **Yes** or **Not now**.
16. **The coach view (0007).** The owner's decision, after the school feed: Pat is Jordan's tutor and also his executive-function coach, and the owner was told (without objection) that Pat's link would also show his points and rewards, his focus sessions, his own calendar and his daily tasks, never the rest of the family. Default: **coach view off by default; turn it on for the tutor** (a switch on each tutor link, the parents only). Pat may leave the parents a note; nothing more (no writes to points, rewards or tasks). §20.

### 1.3 Done (the owner looked, 2026-10-31)

This decided whether 0006's school feed was worth building. **Confirmed:** on a parent's own login, "Jordan's Calendars" → **Calendar** has a feed button opening **WebCal Feed for External Calendar**, with **Entire Calendar** and **Individual Filter Feeds** (Assignments, Schedule, Games/Practices, Class Events, Group Events), each copied with right-click → **Copy Link Address**; the feed holds the past 2 months and the upcoming 12, and setting changes can take up to an hour (§11.1). No student login is needed. If the Assignments feed turns out empty, the one email in §11.2 C asks the school to switch it on.

Typed assignments with the pasted teacher's text work either way.

### 1.4 What the owner does, and what it costs

**Time.** Each step is written out in §16.

| When | What | Minutes |
|---|---|---|
| Now | Look for the school's calendar feed (§1.3) | 3 |
| PR A is live | Paste `0004`, only if v1.2 isn't applied yet | 5 |
| PR A is live | Paste `0005` | 5 |
| PR A is live | Turn homework on for Jordan, set his homework time, write three rewards, send Pat the link | 10 |
| PR A is live | Try it with Jordan: one assignment, one focus block, his start cue; set a Homework Focus and a repeating alarm on his phone | 10 |
| PR B is live (optional) | Anthropic account with a $15 monthly limit and a key; paste the key into Supabase; words never to send; **Test**; turn the AI on | 15 |
| 0006 is live | Paste `0006` and `cron.sql`; copy his school links on a parent's login; the first-import check | 15 |
| After a term (optional) | Look at the points with Jordan | 10 |

Required for v1.3: **about 25 minutes** (30 if 0004 isn't applied yet). With the AI: about 40.

**Money, per month.**

| What | Cost |
|---|---|
| The AI planner (Anthropic, PR B) | **about $3–4**: 20 assignments with two revisions each, about 6 cents a draft. **Never more than $15**, the limit set at Anthropic. Paid ahead: $10 of credits lasts about three months, or turn on auto-reload. |
| Supabase, GitHub Pages, Resend | **unchanged**: small tables, a few Edge Function calls a day, no new emails |
| Blackbaud (0006) | nothing |
| **Total new** | **about $3–4 a month; $0 without the AI** |

## 2. What ships

### 2.1 PR A: v1.3 "Homework" (migration 0005, schema 5)

| Part | What |
|---|---|
| `supabase/migrations/0005_homework.sql` | Eleven tables (`homework_kids`, `homework_times`, `tutor_links`, `assignments`, `homework_steps`, `focus_sessions`, `rewards`, `reward_asks`, `points_ledger`, `homework_use`, `ai_runs`); three `household` columns; twenty-six new public functions (thirteen for signed-in pages, eleven for My day and Pat's link, two for `hub-jobs`) and twenty-seven new private ones; replaces `hub_schema_version()` and `get_person_day` (§3). The AI's SQL side (`ai_runs`, the AI columns, `homework_ai_preview`, `homework_ai_start`, `homework_ai_finish` and their helpers) is in it and tested by the database suite; nothing calls it until PR B. |
| `assets/plan.js`, `assets/css/plan.css` (new) | Shared by the Homework page and `day.html`, as `shoplist.js` is shared by Food and My day: the plan editor, the starter plans, the week strip, the two bars, timer arithmetic, Pat's mode, and their words in English and Spanish. **No Supabase calls of its own:** each page hands it an `api`. No AI part yet. |
| `assets/modules/homework.js`, `assets/css/homework.css` (new) | The Homework page (§9.1), without the AI parts. |
| `assets/modules/today.js` | The parents' Homework lines, on schema 5 only (§9.3). |
| `assets/day.js`, `assets/css/day.css`, `day.html` | Jordan's views (`#homework`, `#focus`, `#points`, `#plan/<id>`) and Pat's mode for `?h=` (§9.5, §9.6). `day.html` loads `plan.js` after `shoplist.js`, before `day.js`, and `plan.css` after `day.css`. |
| `index.html` | `plan.css` and `homework.css`; script tags for `plan.js` (after `shoplist.js`) and `homework.js` (after `food.js`). |
| `index.html`, `day.html` | One new `?v=` on every script and stylesheet, in both files. |
| Tests and docs | §14, §15. |
| **Not changed** | Everything under `supabase/functions/`; `assets/hub.js` (the tab is in More by its `nav`; More draws `ICON.page` for a tab with no icon, `hub.js:1168`); `supabase/cron.sql`; `digest_for` and `private.digest_for_v3`, `private.calendar_desired` and its `_v3` body, `calendar_plan`, `day_text`, `agenda`, `private.person_items`; tasks and every task function; `shoplist.js`, `share.html`, `gantt.js`. |

The deploy is v1.2's: the site goes live, then the owner pastes 0005 (§16.1).

|---|---|

The deploy is v1.1's for `hub-jobs`: the site and the function go live; there is no paste (§16.2).

### 2.3 0006, the next release (schema 6)

Blackbaud feeds read hourly by a `school` action in `hub-jobs` (`school.ts`, `ical.ts`), with a fifth `cron.sql` job and no new secret; "New from school" on Jordan's screen; schedule and games feeds as busy time; the parents' status card with Add link, Replace and Remove. §11 and §12 are its contract. Later, each additive: the homework line in `day_text`; Jordan drafting his own plan and Pat's AI "What's missing?"; pace applied in placement (§12.6).

### 2.4 Schema ranges

Only `homework` is new: `HomeHub.register({ name: 'homework', schema: { min: 5, max: 99 }, nav: { label: 'Homework', order: 37 } })`, not primary, so it is under More on phones (the bottom bar stays at six cells) and a tab between Food (35) and Projects (40) on desktop. Today's Homework lines check `core.schemaVersion >= 5` and make **no** request below. `day.js` has no schema version: Jordan's card appears only when `get_person_day` returns `homework`, which 0004's body never does; Pat's mode calls functions that exist only on 5, and before that the link answers "This link doesn't work yet" (PostgREST's "function not found", `PGRST202`). The AI parts appear only with PR B's code; before it, nothing on any page mentions the AI.

### 2.5 Jordan's next assignment, with v1.3 (made up)

- **Sun Nov 1.** Alex types "Science project: river poster", Science, due Fri Nov 13, and pastes the teacher's sheet (copied from a photo with Live Text).
- **Mon Nov 2, tutoring.** Pat opens the link on Pat's own laptop. The assignment shows "No plan yet". Pat opens **What Claude will see**, reads it with Jordan (the class, the dates, the teacher's text with the names the hub knows taken out, and his free minutes per day), and taps **Send to Claude**. About 20 to 40 seconds later a draft of nine steps appears, labelled "Suggested by AI (Claude). Check it with Jordan.", with its days already placed in his homework time (Mon–Thu 4:30–5:15, Sun 5:00–5:45, less Tuesday's piano at 5:00), every work step by Wed Nov 11 and **Turn it in** on Fri Nov 13. They rename two steps (each change locks the step), tap **Smaller steps** once, and Pat taps **Give Jordan this plan**.
- **Mon Nov 2, 4:31pm.** Jordan's My day says "Homework · 4:30 · 2 steps · 30 min · Next: Copy the requirements into your notes [Start · 10]" and "15 points to 15 minutes of extra game time". He taps **Start**: his own cue shows for a moment ("When I've had my snack, I open my notes doc."), then the ring, "On time · +10 once 5 minutes are done", and "★ 0 +0 so far", which counts up a point a minute. Done after 7 minutes, he taps **Done** → **Keep going: Find 2 sources** and the same block runs on; at 10 minutes, a chime and his points at once: +10, and +10 for starting on time, the counter ticking up to ★ 20 with "+20". Next a 20-minute block on the sources; at the end he taps **More to do** (one source still to check): +20 all the same, and "You can get 15 minutes of extra game time now!"
- **Wed Nov 4.** He ticks the milestone "Outline with 3 main points". His screen says "+20 now · +20 more when Pat checks it", and the 20 are his at once. Thursday Pat looks at the outline and taps **Looks done**, with "Clear outline. The third point is the strongest." The other +20, and the note shows under the milestone on his screen.
- **Mon Nov 9.** Stuck on the draft, he taps **I'm stuck** → **Make it smaller** → "Write the first sentence" (10 minutes); the running block moves to it. Later, **Ask Pat**: "Does the poster need a title?" Pat answers on the link that evening; Morgan sees the question and the answer on the Homework page.
- **Tue Nov 10.** He adds "Math · Worksheet 5.1 · due Wed" himself with **+ Homework** and "15 minutes": it is tonight's first step, and **Now**.
- **Fri Nov 13.** He ticks **Turn it in**; the assignment is done.
- **Thu Nov 12.** After his block he taps **Get it now** on "15 minutes of extra game time · 15 · instant", then **Yes, get it · −15**: "Got it: 15 minutes of extra game time. Tell a parent." Alex's Today shows "Jordan cashed in: 15 minutes of extra game time · 15 · 5:02pm".
- **Sun Nov 15.** He has 318 points and asks for "Pick the movie · 200". Morgan's Today shows "Jordan asks: Pick the movie · 200 · balance 318 (this week: +150 focus and on time, +40 milestones ticked, +40 milestones checked by Pat) · Yes · Not now". She taps **Yes**.

### 2.6 0007, the coach view (schema 7)

`tutor_links.coach` (off by default), `coach_notes`, `coach_view_by_token` and `coach_note_by_token` for the link, `set_tutor_coach` and `coach_note_done` for the parents; Pat's link's Coach section, the switch and the notes on the Homework page, and a line on Jordan's screen. No Edge Function, no `cron.sql`. §20 is its contract.

### 2.7 0008, the first-import check when nothing is due yet (schema 8)

A link read over a weekend or a holiday can hold only past assignments (the reader keeps those due from 14 days back), and the first-import check showed only ones still to come, so it was empty and the page had nothing to ask: until a parent answers, nothing from the link reaches Jordan or Pat. `0008_school_check.sql` replaces `school_feed_status` (same signature): its check fills up with the latest past items, each marked `past`, and `hub_schema_version()` returns 8. It also replaces `set_school_feed` (same signature) so that a link removed and added again starts as not read, instead of keeping the old link's last read. The Homework page says "was due" for those and, when the check is empty, why (not read yet, read with nothing in it, or every item past on a database before 0008), and, for a link read before, whether it is paused or needs Replace before anything new can show. No Edge Function, no `cron.sql`. §11.2 step 7, §12 item 2, §12.8 and §16.6 are its contract.

## 3. Database: migration `0005_homework.sql`

A fresh install is `schema.sql`, then `0002` to `0005`. Like the others, it runs as one transaction and is additive, and every rule in v2-design §3 applies:
- RLS on every table.
- `set search_path = ''` and schema-qualified names in every function.
- `revoke execute … from public, anon` on every new function, public and private, then a grant to whoever calls it. (0002's blanket revoke on `private` ran once.)
- `revoke all … from public, anon, authenticated` on every new table before its grants (Supabase's default privileges would give everything, truncate included).
- Audit columns come from `private.stamp()`. Every time the functions write is `private.now()`.
- **The migration seeds no rows** in the new tables. The same-output check depends on it.

### 3.1 Guard and version

```sql
begin;
do $$
begin
  if to_regprocedure('public.hub_schema_version()') is null then
    raise exception 'Home Hub v2 (migration 0002) isn''t installed. Run 0002, 0003 and 0004 first. Nothing was changed.';
  end if;
  if public.hub_schema_version() < 4 then
    raise exception 'Meals and shopping (migration 0004) isn''t installed (hub_schema_version() is %). Run 0004_meals_and_shopping.sql first. Nothing was changed.',
                    public.hub_schema_version();
  end if;
  if public.hub_schema_version() >= 5 then
    raise exception 'Homework (migration 0005) is already installed (hub_schema_version() is %). Nothing was changed.',
                    public.hub_schema_version();
  end if;
  if to_regclass('public.homework_kids') is not null then
    raise exception 'Homework (migration 0005) is already installed, but hub_schema_version() was set back to 4. To switch it on again, make hub_schema_version() return 5 (docs/v1.3-homework.md §16.3). Nothing was changed.';
  end if;
  -- ... and the shape check below.
end;
$$;
```

The set-back check comes before the shape check, so a database rolled back as §16.3 says gets the right message. The shape check, in the same block: `public.get_person_day` and `private.digest_for_v3` exist with 0004's argument types, and `public.shopping_items` exists (0004 is as it left the database); otherwise "This database isn't as migration 0004 left it. Nothing was changed."

The migration ends with `create or replace function public.hub_schema_version()` returning 5 (`create or replace` keeps the grants), then `commit;`. 0004's own guard (`>= 4`) still says "already installed" on a version-5 database, and 0003's (`<> 2`) too, so `tests/db/run.sh`'s step 4 keeps passing.

### 3.2 Tables and columns

```
homework_kids (homework is on for this kid: one row per kid, made by a parent)
  kid_id         uuid PK → people on delete cascade        -- a kid (save_homework_kid checks the role)
  active         boolean not null default true             -- off: no card on his My day, his tutor links answer null,
                                                           -- the AI refuses; nothing deleted
  points_on      boolean not null default true             -- off: nothing earned; points, rewards and asks hidden on My day
  focus_minutes  int not null default 10 check (focus_minutes in (10, 15, 20, 25))   -- the length offered first
  cue            text check (cue is null or char_length(cue) between 1 and 120)
                                                           -- his start cue, in his words: "When I've had my snack, I open my notes doc"
  if_stuck       text check (if_stuck is null or char_length(if_stuck) between 1 and 120)
                                                           -- his if-then: "If I'm stuck for 5 minutes, I'll write the question for Pat"
  pts_focus      int not null default 1 check (pts_focus between 0 and 5)          -- per focused minute of a block
  pts_on_time    int not null default 10 check (pts_on_time between 0 and 50)
  pts_milestone  int not null default 40 check (pts_milestone between 0 and 100)   -- half at his tick, the rest at the check
  pts_day_cap    int not null default 60 check (pts_day_cap between 0 and 300)     -- focus and on-time points a day
  pts_milestone_week int not null default 160 check (pts_milestone_week between 0 and 400)
                                                           -- milestone points a household week (Mon–Sun), both halves
  instant_day    int not null default 2 check (instant_day between 0 and 10)       -- instant rewards he may cash a day
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(), created_by text,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(), updated_by text
  triggers (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE; name order, so stamp runs before tidy):
    homework_kids_stamp (private.stamp()); homework_kids_tidy (private.homework_kids_tidy(): cue and if_stuck
    through private.clean_text; '' becomes null)
  RLS: members select; admins update. Insert through save_homework_kid; delete through erase_homework.

homework_times (which repeating events are a kid's homework time)
  event_id uuid PK → events on delete cascade
  kid_id   uuid not null → homework_kids on delete cascade
  index homework_times_kid on (kid_id)
  RLS: members select; admins insert, delete. (The event itself is changed on the Calendar page, as any event.)

tutor_links (a tutor's way in: one kid's homework and nothing else; at most 3 per kid)
  id           uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()
  kid_id       uuid not null → homework_kids on delete cascade
  token        text not null unique default encode(extensions.gen_random_bytes(16), 'hex') check (token ~ '^[0-9a-f]{32}$')
  label        text not null check (char_length(label) between 1 and 60)          -- "Pat Lee, tutor"
  language     text not null default 'en' check (language in ('en', 'es'))
  active       boolean not null default true                                       -- Off: the link answers null at once
  expires_on   date not null                                -- today + 180 unless a parent sets it; at most today + 400
  can_ai       boolean not null default true                -- may send an assignment to Claude (while household.homework_ai is on)
  last_used_at timestamptz                                  -- the last call through the link, to the minute
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(), created_by text,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(), updated_by text
  index tutor_links_kid on (kid_id)
  triggers: tutor_links_stamp (private.stamp(), BEFORE INSERT); tutor_links_stamp_change (private.stamp(), BEFORE UPDATE
    when ((to_jsonb(old) - 'last_used_at') is distinct from (to_jsonb(new) - 'last_used_at')), so a read through the link
    never changes updated_at); tutor_links_tidy (private.tutor_links_tidy(): label through clean_text)
  RLS: admins select (to copy and send the link). Writes through save_tutor_link and new_tutor_token.

assignments (an assignment from school, or a lesson a parent or Pat plans)
  id          uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()                -- made by the page for adds from Jordan's and Pat's links
  kid_id      uuid not null → homework_kids on delete cascade
  kind        text not null default 'assignment' check (kind in ('assignment', 'lesson'))
  added_by    text not null default 'family' check (added_by in ('family', 'tutor', 'kid'))   -- "added by Jordan"
  title       text not null check (char_length(title) between 1 and 120)
  subject     text check (subject is null or char_length(subject) between 1 and 40)          -- "Science"
  due_date    date not null                                 -- a lesson's "do by" day
  details     text check (details is null or char_length(details) between 1 and 4000)
                                                            -- the teacher's text, pasted, line breaks kept: what the AI reads;
                                                            -- emptied 30 days after due_date (§13.3)
  stretch     text check (stretch is null or char_length(stretch) between 1 and 160)
                                                            -- an optional "interesting angle" question for him, from a used
                                                            -- AI draft or typed; earns nothing and is never a step
  status      text not null default 'open' check (status in ('open', 'done', 'dropped'))
  done_at     timestamptz                                   -- set by the tidy trigger when status becomes 'done'
  dropped_at  timestamptz                                   -- set by the tidy trigger when status becomes 'dropped' (§13.3 counts from it)
  given_at    timestamptz, given_by text                    -- the plan is on his My day since, by (an email,
                                                            -- "Pat Lee, tutor (tutor link)", or "Jordan (My day)" for Quick)
  plan_rev    int not null default 0                        -- +1 on every change to its steps (his ticks and moves too)
  ai_state    text check (ai_state in ('running', 'ready', 'failed'))
  ai_stop     text                                          -- the last run's fixed code when it failed (§6.9)
  ai_draft    jsonb                                         -- the last AI draft, checked and placed; never the plan (§6.10)
  ai_prev     jsonb                                         -- the draft before it (one Undo)
  ai_at       timestamptz                                   -- when ai_draft was written; both cleared 30 days after
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(), created_by text,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(), updated_by text
  check ((status = 'done') = (done_at is not null))
  check ((status = 'dropped') = (dropped_at is not null))
  check ((given_at is null) = (given_by is null))
  index assignments_kid on (kid_id, status, due_date)
  triggers: assignments_stamp (private.stamp()); assignments_tidy (private.assignments_tidy(): title and subject
    through private.clean_text, details through private.clean_block; done_at and dropped_at from status)
  RLS: members select; admins insert, update, delete (column grants). Pat's link writes through its functions.

homework_steps (an assignment's plan: its steps in order)
  id              uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()         -- Make it smaller: the phone's key
  assignment_id   uuid not null → assignments on delete cascade
  pos             int not null check (pos between 1 and 20)
  kind            text not null default 'step' check (kind in ('start', 'step', 'milestone', 'check', 'turn_in'))
  title           text not null check (char_length(title) between 3 and 80)     -- "Find 2 sources and save their links"
  done_looks_like text check (done_looks_like is null or char_length(done_looks_like) between 1 and 160)
  minutes         int not null check (minutes between 5 and 60)
  day             date not null                             -- the day it's planned for
  moved_from      date                                      -- the day before his last "Not today"
  source          text not null default 'typed' check (source in ('typed', 'template', 'ai'))
                                                            -- 'typed' once a person changes it: a revision keeps it ("locked")
  done_at         timestamptz, done_by text                 -- "Jordan (My day)", "Pat Lee, tutor (tutor link)", an email
  checked_at      timestamptz, checked_by text              -- a milestone Pat or a parent looked at: its points are paid then
  not_yet_at      timestamptz                               -- Not yet; cleared by his next tick
  coach_note      text check (coach_note is null or char_length(coach_note) between 1 and 160)
                                                            -- Pat's or a parent's words with Looks done (optional) or Not yet
                                                            -- (required): "Add a source for the second point."
  coach_note_by   text check (coach_note_by in ('tutor', 'family')), coach_note_at timestamptz
  question        text check (question is null or char_length(question) between 1 and 200)   -- Ask Pat, from his My day
  question_at     timestamptz
  answer          text check (answer is null or char_length(answer) between 1 and 200)       -- Pat's or a parent's answer
  answer_by       text, answer_at timestamptz               -- answer_by: the writer's label (an email, or the link's label)
  qa_log          jsonb not null default '[]'               -- earlier questions and answers on this step, with who and when
                                                            -- (at most 10): nothing Pat wrote to him is lost
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(), updated_by text
                                                            -- set by plan_save and step_act (private.now(), the writer's
                                                            -- label); no stamp trigger
  check ((done_at is null) = (done_by is null))
  check ((checked_at is null) = (checked_by is null))
  check (checked_at is null or (kind = 'milestone' and done_at is not null))
  check ((coach_note is null) = (coach_note_at is null) and (coach_note is null) = (coach_note_by is null))
  index homework_steps_assignment on (assignment_id, pos)
  index homework_steps_open_day on (day) where done_at is null
  RLS: members select. No write policy and no write grant: the functions in §3.5 write.

focus_sessions (one focus block on the timer)
  id            uuid PK                                     -- made by the phone, so a resent Start finds it (only its kid's)
  kid_id        uuid not null → homework_kids on delete cascade
  assignment_id uuid → assignments on delete set null
  step_id       uuid → homework_steps on delete set null
  minutes       int not null check (minutes in (10, 15, 20, 25))           -- the length chosen
  with_whom     text not null default 'alone' check (with_whom in ('alone', 'tutor', 'parent'))   -- recorded, not rewarded
  started_at    timestamptz not null                        -- private.now()
  ends_at       timestamptz not null                        -- started_at + minutes
  ended_at      timestamptz
  result        text check (result in ('done', 'more', 'stopped', 'time', 'expired'))
                                                            -- 'time': the ring ran out and the phone ended it (paid);
                                                            -- his answer (done, more, stopped) replaces it, paying nothing more
  seen_minutes  int check (seen_minutes is null or seen_minutes between 0 and 25)   -- whole minutes the server saw, at most minutes
  on_time       boolean not null default false              -- the day's first block, started in the on-time window (§7.1)
  points        int not null default 0                      -- what it earned (focus and on-time), for the record
  note          text check (note is null or char_length(note) between 1 and 120)   -- "what I got done", optional
  check ((ended_at is null) = (result is null))
  check (ends_at = started_at + make_interval(mins => minutes))
  unique index focus_sessions_open on (kid_id) where ended_at is null             -- one open block per kid
  index focus_sessions_kid on (kid_id, started_at desc)
  RLS: members select. No write policy, no write grant.

rewards (what points buy: the parents' list, and Jordan's suggestions)
  id         uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()              -- a suggestion: the phone's key
  kid_id     uuid not null → homework_kids on delete cascade
  title      text not null check (char_length(title) between 1 and 60)          -- "Choose Friday's dinner"
  cost       int check (cost is null or cost between 1 and 10000)               -- null: a suggestion nobody has priced yet
  active     boolean not null default true
  instant    boolean not null default false              -- he cashes it on the spot (no parent's yes), at most instant_day a day
  suggested  boolean not null default false                                      -- Jordan suggested it
  sort_order int not null default 0
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(), created_by text,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(), updated_by text
  check (cost is not null or not active)
  triggers: rewards_stamp (private.stamp()); rewards_tidy (private.rewards_tidy(): title through clean_text)
  RLS: members select; admins insert, update, delete (column grants). His suggestions through reward_by_token.

reward_asks (his trades: each asked by him, decided by a parent; or an instant reward he cashed)
  id         uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()              -- the phone's key
  kid_id     uuid not null → homework_kids on delete cascade
  reward_id  uuid → rewards on delete set null
  title      text not null, cost int not null               -- copied when asked, so a later edit doesn't change a trade
  status     text not null default 'waiting' check (status in ('waiting', 'approved', 'declined', 'cancelled', 'cashed'))
                                                            -- 'cashed': an instant reward he took on the spot (decided at once, by him)
  asked_at   timestamptz not null
  decided_at timestamptz, decided_by text
  note       text check (note is null or char_length(note) between 1 and 120)  -- a parent's word with a decline
  unique index reward_asks_waiting on (kid_id, reward_id) where status = 'waiting'    -- a resent ask finds the waiting one
  index reward_asks_kid on (kid_id, asked_at desc)
  RLS: members select. Writes through reward_by_token and decide_reward.

points_ledger (every point earned, given, voided and traded)
  id         uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()              -- given points: the parent's page's key
  kid_id     uuid not null → homework_kids on delete cascade
  at         timestamptz not null                           -- private.now()
  day        date not null                                  -- the household's day it counts for: a block's start day for
                                                            -- 'focus' and 'on_time' (the daily cap), else today
  kind       text not null check (kind in ('focus', 'on_time', 'milestone_done', 'milestone', 'given', 'void', 'trade'))
                                                            -- 'milestone_done': half a milestone, at his tick; 'milestone': the rest, at the check
  delta      int not null check (delta <> 0 and delta between -10000 and 1000)
  session_id uuid → focus_sessions on delete set null
  step_id    uuid → homework_steps on delete set null
  ask_id     uuid → reward_asks on delete set null
  void_of    uuid → points_ledger on delete set null        -- the entry a 'void' cancels
  note       text check (note is null or char_length(note) between 1 and 120)   -- the step's or reward's title, a parent's reason
  who        text not null                                  -- "Jordan (My day)", "Pat Lee, tutor (tutor link)", a parent's email
  check ((kind in ('void', 'trade')) = (delta < 0))
  unique index points_focus_once on (session_id) where kind = 'focus'
  unique index points_on_time_once on (kid_id, day) where kind = 'on_time'
  unique index points_milestone_once on (step_id) where kind = 'milestone'
  unique index points_milestone_done_once on (step_id) where kind = 'milestone_done'
  unique index points_void_once on (void_of) where kind = 'void'
  index points_ledger_kid on (kid_id, at desc)
  RLS: members select. No write policy, no write grant: private.award and give_points write. Rows are never changed;
  the only deletes are a milestone check taken back within 15 minutes (a mis-tap, §8.4), and the half a milestone
  paid at his tick when he unticks it himself (§8.4).

homework_use (what each link did to homework today: the caps in §13.1)
  actor_id uuid                                             -- the kid (his My day) or the tutor link
  day      date                                             -- the household's day
  what     text check (what in ('writes', 'starts'))
  n        int not null default 0
  primary key (actor_id, day, what)
  RLS on, no policies, no grants: only the definer function private.count_homework writes and reads it.

ai_runs (one row per AI request: who, when, what it cost; never the prompt or the answer)
  id            uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()
  kid_id        uuid → homework_kids on delete cascade       -- null for Test the AI planner
  assignment_id uuid → assignments on delete set null
  link_id       uuid → tutor_links on delete set null
  kind          text not null check (kind in ('plan', 'revise', 'test'))
  asked_by      text not null                               -- a parent's email, or "Pat Lee, tutor (tutor link)"
  key           uuid not null unique                        -- the page's key: a resend gets this run
  status        text not null default 'running' check (status in ('running', 'ready', 'failed'))
  stop          text                                        -- a fixed code (§6.9)
  model         text                                        -- the model that answered (a fallback names its own)
  started_at    timestamptz not null, lease_until timestamptz not null, finished_at timestamptz
  input_tokens int, cache_read_tokens int, cache_write_tokens int, output_tokens int
  cents         numeric(8, 2)                               -- an estimate, from fixed prices in homework.ts
  index ai_runs_started on (started_at)
  index ai_runs_assignment on (assignment_id, started_at)
  RLS: admins select. Writes through homework_ai_start and homework_ai_finish.

household (three new columns; members read, admins update, as today)
  homework_ai          boolean not null default false       -- the AI planner; off until a parent turns it on
  homework_ai_month    int not null default 150 check (homework_ai_month between 0 and 500)   -- AI runs a month, at most
  homework_never_send  text[] not null default '{}' check (cardinality(homework_never_send) <= 20)
                                                            -- words the AI must never see (the school's name, teachers'
                                                            -- names): each 2 to 40 characters, one line
  trigger household_homework_check (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE; private.household_homework_check()): each never-send word
    2 to 40 characters on one line, else 22023 "Words never to send: each 2 to 40 characters, one line."; homework_ai on with
    no never-send word → 22023 "Add the school's name to Words never to send first."
```

Notes:
- **Why `tutor_links` is a table, not a column on `homework_kids`:** an end date, a "last used" time and a per-link AI switch, and a second tutor later, without a migration. It is never a person: every existing token function looks up `people.day_token` only, so a tutor token opens nothing else.
- **Why `homework_use` and not v1.2's `my_day_use`:** `count_my_day` is keyed on a person and hard-codes `'changes'` and `'ticks'` (`0004:473-497`), and Pat's link is not a person. A sibling table touches nothing of v1.2. Counters on `homework_kids` would bump its `updated_at` and trip the Homework page's save check (and so would `last_used_at` on `tutor_links`, which is why its stamp trigger skips that column).
- **A resend changes nothing**, as in v1.2 (`docs/v1.2-kitchen.md` §8), without v1.2's key table: every write that adds a row takes its id from the page (a block, a smaller step, an assignment Jordan or Pat adds, a suggestion, an ask, given points), and a resend finds that row and answers it. A row with that id that belongs to another kid or link gets the same refusal as an unknown id. Every other write is a "set" that a resend repeats harmlessly (done, ask, answer, check), carries the state it was made on (**Not today** names the day it moves the step from; plan saves need `p_rev`), or finds the decision already made (a trade).
- **Why the AI draft is `jsonb` on the assignment:** `homework_steps` holds only plans people saved. A draft that is never the plan can't reach Jordan's My day by mistake, and an Undo is one more value.
- **Why `pos` has no unique index:** a save rewrites positions in one statement; reads order by `pos, id`.
- **Homework time stays an event.** `homework_times` only says which events count. Deleting the event on the Calendar page removes the link (cascade), and the Homework page then says "No homework time set".
- **The tutor label "Pat Lee, tutor"** is plain text. `private.short_name` and contractor lookups never see it.

**Table grants.** As 0003 and 0004 do:
```
revoke all on public.homework_kids, public.homework_times, public.tutor_links, public.assignments,
              public.homework_steps, public.focus_sessions, public.rewards, public.reward_asks,
              public.points_ledger, public.homework_use, public.ai_runs
  from public, anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.homework_kids, public.homework_times, public.tutor_links, public.assignments,
                public.homework_steps, public.focus_sessions, public.rewards, public.reward_asks,
                public.points_ledger, public.ai_runs to authenticated;          -- RLS: tutor_links and ai_runs admins only
grant update (active, points_on, focus_minutes, pts_focus, pts_on_time, pts_milestone, pts_day_cap, pts_milestone_week, instant_day)
  on public.homework_kids to authenticated;
grant insert (event_id, kid_id), delete on public.homework_times to authenticated;
grant insert (kid_id, kind, title, subject, due_date, details, stretch, status),
      update (kind, title, subject, due_date, details, stretch, status), delete on public.assignments to authenticated;
grant insert (kid_id, title, cost, active, instant, sort_order), update (title, cost, active, instant, sort_order), delete
  on public.rewards to authenticated;
revoke all on public.homework_use from service_role;
```
Row-level security narrows every write to admins. `service_role` keeps Supabase's defaults on the rest (`hub-jobs` calls only definer functions anyway). `cue` and `if_stuck` are written only by Jordan's link (`homework_prefs_by_token`): they are his words. The household's three columns follow its policies (members read, admins update), as 0004's two did.

### 3.3 Who can do what

| | Signed out (anon) | Signed in, not family | Sam (adult) | Alex, Morgan (parents) | Jordan's link (kid) | Pat's link (tutor) | Taylor's link (helper) | `hub-jobs` (service_role) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `homework_kids` | no privilege | 0 rows | read | read; turn on and off; points on and off; point values | his first length, start cue, if-stuck line (read and set his own) | his first name only (the first word of his name) | nothing | — |
| `homework_times` and the events | no privilege | 0 rows | read; the events as any event | read; add, remove; the events on the Calendar page | his homework time, as events on My day | the times and "Busy" blocks, read-only, no titles | as any event she's on | — |
| `tutor_links` | no privilege | 0 rows | 0 rows | read (the token, to send it); make, change, New link, Off, Extend | nothing | its own label, language and end date | nothing | read through `homework_ai_start` |
| `assignments` | no privilege | 0 rows | read | read, add, edit, mark done, drop, delete; Undo or Dismiss the AI draft (`homework_ai_draft`) | his open ones and the last 3 days' done ones: title, class, due date, the stretch question; add his own, **Quick** on one with no plan, drop one he added; never the teacher's text or the AI draft | the kid's: read with the teacher's text, add, edit, mark done, drop (never delete); Undo or Dismiss the AI draft | nothing | the AI fields, through `homework_ai_finish` |
| `homework_steps` | no privilege | 0 rows | read | read; plan, give, edit; check milestones with a note, Not yet with a note; answer questions; clear an answer or a note | the steps of plans given to him: tick, Undo his tick for 15 minutes, Not today, Re-plan, Make it smaller, Ask Pat, reorder and move plain steps | the kid's: plan, give, edit; check milestones with a note, Not yet with a note; answer questions (change its own answer for 15 minutes) | nothing | — |
| `focus_sessions` | no privilege | 0 rows | read | read | start, switch step, end his own | the kid's finished ones: day, length, whether it was with Pat, result, his notes; never a block running now, never "alone" | nothing | — |
| `points_ledger` | no privilege | 0 rows | read | read; give points; void an entry with a reason | his balance, held points and last 10 entries | **nothing** | nothing | — |
| `rewards`, `reward_asks` | no privilege | 0 rows | read | the list; price his suggestions; Yes or Not now on his asks | the active rewards; ask, cancel his own ask; suggest one | **nothing** | nothing | — |
| `ai_runs` | no privilege | 0 rows | 0 rows | read (who asked, when, result, estimated cost) | nothing | its own runs' state, through its view | nothing | write, through the two job functions |
| `homework_use` | no privilege | no privilege | no privilege | no privilege | counted by his functions | counted by its functions | — | — |
| `household.homework_*` | — | — | read | read, change | — | — | — | read |

Every policy is `private.is_member()` or `private.is_admin()`, as everywhere else. What a link sees is decided inside the token functions, which are SECURITY DEFINER and scope everything themselves. A tutor link's functions never read `people` beyond the first word of Jordan's name (`split_part(name, ' ', 1)`, never `private.short_name`, which gives the whole name when two active people share a first name, `0002:1148-1156`), and never read tasks, meals, the list, the directory, contractors or `household` beyond the AI switch.

### 3.4 What each answer carries

**The card** (`get_person_day`'s new key, `private.homework_card`), only for a kid with an active `homework_kids` row:
```
homework: { next: { id, title, assignment, minutes } | null,   -- the same step as Now (below): today's first open step,
                                                               -- or the first earlier one when today has none
            length: 10,                                        -- his first length, for the card's Start
            steps: 3, minutes: 55,                             -- today's open steps and their minutes (earlier ones not counted)
            earlier: 2,                                        -- open steps from earlier days; left out when none or no
                                                               -- homework time today
            window: { starts: '16:30', ends: '17:15' },        -- today's homework time; left out when there is none today
            session: { ends_in_s: 754 },                       -- a block running, from the server's clock; left out when none
            answer_due: true,                                  -- a block paid when its ring ran out, his answer still to
                                                               -- come (the view's answer_due); left out when none
            points: 118,                                       -- left out when points are off
            goal: { next: { id, title, cost, need: 6 } | null,   -- the next-reward bar (as the view's goal, below); left
                    now: { id, title, cost, instant } | null } }  -- out when points are off or he has no priced reward
```

**Jordan's view** (`homework_by_token`, and every answer of his writers, `private.homework_view_kid`):
```
{ language: 'en', as_of: '2026-11-02T16:31', today: '2026-11-02', server_now: '2026-11-02T21:31:04.512Z',
  me: { first_name: 'Jordan', tutor: 'Pat' | null, cue, if_stuck, length: 10, lengths: [10, 15, 20, 25], points_on: true },
                                                                        -- tutor: the first word of his active tutor link's label
  window: { starts: '16:30', ends: '17:15' } | null,
  on_time: { from: '16:00', until: '16:40', done: false, points: 10 } | null,  -- around the first free minute (§7.1); left
                                                                        -- out when there is none today or points are off;
                                                                        -- points: the parents' on-time value
  session: { id, step_id, assignment_id, title, minutes, started_at, ends_at, with, on_time,
             earn: { per_minute: 1, on_time: 10, room: 42 } | null } | null,
                                                                        -- earn: what the block pays, for his live count
                                                                        -- (§7.2): per minute seen, its on-time points
                                                                        -- (0 unless on time), and what is left of the cap
                                                                        -- on its start day; null for his own block, with
                                                                        -- points off, or after 24 hours
  answer_due: { block: { id, step_id, assignment_id, minutes, with, started_at, ends_at, ended_at, result: 'time',
                         seen_minutes, on_time, points },
                earned: { focus, on_time, capped } } | null,            -- his latest block, when its ring ran out (paid,
                                                                        -- §7.1) within 24 hours of its start and he hasn't
                                                                        -- said how it went: as focus_by_token's End answered
                                                                        -- it, so a reload asks him again (§9.6)
  now: step | null,                                                     -- as the card's next
  tonight: [step], load: { planned: 45, free: 45 },                     -- today's steps
  earlier: [step],                                                      -- open steps from earlier days, on a day with homework
                                                                        -- time; [] otherwise
  assignments: [{ id, kind, title, subject, due_date, status, state: 'none' | 'planning' | 'given', plan_rev,
                  added_by: 'kid' | 'tutor' | 'family', given_by: 'tutor' | 'family' | 'kid' | null, ai_help: true,
                  stretch, behind: { steps: 3, milestone: { title, day } | null } | null,
                  minutes_left, time_left, horizon, no_time, next_milestone: { title, day } | null, steps: [step] }],
                                                                        -- open ones by due date, then done in the last 3 days;
                                                                        -- steps only once the plan was given (state 'given')
  points: { balance, held, today, capped, cash_left: 2 } | null,       -- null when points are off; no daily cap shown;
                                                                        -- cash_left: instant rewards he may still get today
  rewards: [{ id, title, cost, instant, can_ask, can_cash }] | null,    -- can_cash: instant, his free points cover it and
                                                                        -- cash_left > 0
  goal: { next: { id, title, cost, need } | null,                       -- the cheapest reward he can't afford yet (never one
          now: { id, title, cost, instant } | null } | null,            -- he asked for: its points are held), and the points
                                                                        -- it needs; now: the dearest he can get now (an
                                                                        -- instant one he may cash, or one to ask for)
  asks: [{ id, reward_id, title, cost, status, asked_at, decided_at, note }] | null,   -- waiting, and decided (approved,
                                                                        -- declined, cancelled or cashed) in 7 days
  recent: [{ day, kind, delta, note }] | null,                          -- the last 10 ledger rows
  pace: { ratio: 1.3, steps: 9 } | null,                                -- null with fewer than 5 steps worked
  week: { focused: 100, last_week: 75 } }                               -- focused minutes, this week and last
step = { id, assignment_id, assignment, kind, title, done_looks_like, minutes, day, from_day, moved, done, undo,
         checked, waiting: 20, not_yet, coach: { by: 'tutor' | 'family', text } | null, question, answer }
                                                                        -- waiting: a milestone's points still to come (the
                                                                        -- check's half once his tick paid the first)
```
No person names but his own and the tutor's first word (`me.tutor`), no emails, no ids of people, no teacher's text, no AI draft, no `note_for_adult`, no tokens. `coach.by` and `given_by` are roles, never names.

**Pat's view** (`tutor_view_by_token`, `private.homework_view_tutor`):
```
{ language: 'en', as_of, today, server_now, tutor: 'Pat Lee, tutor', kid: 'Jordan', ends_on: '2027-04-30',
  ai: { on: true, left_today: 7 } | null,                               -- null when the AI is off or the link can't ask
  to_check: [{ step_id, assignment, title, done_looks_like, day, done_day }],
  to_talk_about: [{ assignment_id, title, milestone, planned_day }],    -- milestones past their day, or that his steps can't
                                                                        -- reach any more (§4.5 Re-plan)
  questions: [{ step_id, assignment, title, question, question_at, answer }],
  plans: [{ assignment_id, title, state, ai_state }],                   -- AI drafts and plans not yet given
  assignments: [{ id, kind, title, subject, due_date, status, state, added_by, given_by, minutes_left, time_left,
                  horizon, no_time, steps_done, steps, next_milestone }],   -- open, and done or dropped in the last 30 days
  week: [{ day, window: { starts, ends } | null, busy: [{ starts, ends }], free, planned }],   -- 14 days, no titles
  record: [{ day, focused, sessions: [{ minutes, with_tutor, result, step, note }] }],        -- the last 14 days, finished
                                                                                              -- blocks only
  pace: { ratio, steps } | null }
with p_assignment, also:
  assignment: { id, kind, title, subject, due_date, details, stretch, status, plan_rev, given_at, given_by,
                steps: [{ id, pos, kind, title, done_looks_like, minutes, day, source, done, done_by, checked,
                          coach, moved_from, question, answer, qa_log }],
                ai: { state, stop, draft, prev, at } | null, preview: '<what Claude will see>' | null, fits, over, horizon }
with p_part = 'ai' (the poll while a run is open), only:
  { assignment: { id, plan_rev, ai } }
```
`given_by`, `done_by`, `added_by` and the `by` of `qa_log` are only `'kid'`, `'tutor'` or `'family'`: **a parent's email never reaches Pat.** `kid` is the first word of his name. Never: a block running now, whether he worked alone or with a parent, points, rewards, asks (with the coach view on, those three come from `coach_view_by_token` instead, §20.3), other people, family event titles, dinners, the list, phone numbers, email addresses, the school link, any token. With the coach view on, Pat's view also carries `coach: true` (and nothing else new).

**Both views never raise.** With no homework time they answer `no_time: true`, `time_left: 0` and `fits: false` (the page says "No homework time set"); dates past the 8-week horizon are placed inside it and named in `horizon` (§5.4); an AI run still `running` past its lease reads as `ai.state 'failed'` with stop `timeout` (§6.3). Only the writers raise.

### 3.5 Functions

**Page read** (STABLE, SECURITY INVOKER; revoke from public, anon; grant to authenticated). Row-level security decides:
- `homework_week(p_kid uuid, p_from date, p_days int default 14) returns jsonb`: per day `{day, window: {starts, ends} | null, busy: [{starts, ends, title}], free, planned, over}` (§5), `p_days` 1 to 56. Busy titles are the members' own events, which they can read anyway.

**Signed-in actions** (SECURITY DEFINER unless said; revoke from public, anon, service_role; grant to authenticated). Each raises 42501 "Only a parent can change homework." unless `private.is_admin()`; Sam's page is read-only.
- `save_homework_kid(p_kid uuid, p_active boolean) returns void`: makes the row for an active person with role `kid` (22023 "Homework is for kids." otherwise), or turns it on or off.
- `add_homework_time(p_kid uuid, p_weekdays int[], p_start time, p_end time) returns uuid`: SECURITY INVOKER (row-level security decides). One weekly event: title `private.homework_word('homework', <the kid's language>)`, `responsible_id` the kid, `repeat` weekly, `repeat_weekdays` `p_weekdays` (**ISO weekdays, 1 = Monday to 7 = Sunday**, as `events.repeat_weekdays`, `0002:133`; the page converts from JavaScript's `getDay()`, where Sunday is 0), `skip_days_off` true, `start_date` the first day from today whose weekday is in `p_weekdays` (0002's `events_weekdays` trigger adds the start date's own weekday to the series, so a Friday start would add Fridays), then the `homework_times` row. `p_end` after `p_start`, at most 4 hours; 1 to 7 weekdays. Returns the event id.
- `save_homework_plan(p_assignment uuid, p_steps jsonb, p_rev int, p_give boolean default false) returns jsonb` → `private.plan_save(…, 'family', <the caller's email>)` (§4.4). `p_rev` is required (0 for a first plan).
- `homework_step(p_step uuid, p_action text, p_text text default null) returns jsonb` → `private.step_act(…, 'family', …)`: `'check'` (`p_text` an optional note, ≤ 160), `'not_yet'` (`p_text` required, ≤ 160), `'uncheck'`, `'answer'` (≤ 200), `'clear'` (removes an answer or a note, kept in `qa_log`), `'done'`, `'undo'` (§4.5).
- `decide_reward(p_ask uuid, p_approve boolean, p_note text default null) returns jsonb`: Yes (the balance is checked again, then a `'trade'` entry of −cost) or Not now (nothing taken; the note, at most 120 characters, is shown to him) (§8.5). An ask already decided answers as it is.
- `give_points(p_kid uuid, p_points int, p_note text, p_key uuid) returns jsonb`: 1 to 200, with a note he sees ("Great session with Pat"), for things the rules can't see. `p_key` is the ledger row's id, so a resend writes nothing more.
- `void_points(p_entry uuid, p_note text) returns jsonb`: a correction, never a penalty rule. One `focus`, `on_time`, `milestone_done`, `milestone` or `given` entry gets a `'void'` of the same size with a reason he sees. Refused when it would take the balance below zero ("Those points are already spent.").
- `save_tutor_link(p_link uuid, p_kid uuid, p_label text, p_language text, p_expires_on date, p_can_ai boolean, p_active boolean) returns uuid`: makes (`p_link` null) or changes a link. At most 3 per kid; the end date from today to today + 400 (a change may keep the link's own end date, even a past one: Off, On or a new name on a link that has ended, which stays ended); null means today + 180.
- `new_tutor_token(p_link uuid) returns void`: a new token; the old link stops at once.
- `homework_ai_preview(p_assignment uuid, p_kind text default 'plan', p_request text default null, p_answers text[] default null) returns text`: exactly the user message the AI would get for that kind, request and answers (§6.4), the same text `homework_ai_start` hands `hub-jobs`; no run, nothing counted.
- `homework_ai_draft(p_assignment uuid, p_action text) returns jsonb`: **Undo** (`'undo'`: `ai_prev` back as the draft, one level, with its own time) or **Dismiss** (`'dismiss'`: `ai_draft`, `ai_prev` and `ai_at` cleared, and `ai_state` and `ai_stop` unless a run is open) on an assignment's AI draft (§6.10), so either sticks for everyone who sees the draft. Nothing to undo or dismiss changes nothing. Answers `{assignment_id, changed}`. 22023 "Choose undo or dismiss." for another action, "That assignment is gone." for an unknown one.
- `erase_homework(p_kid uuid, p_confirm text) returns jsonb`: with `p_confirm = 'ERASE'`, deletes the kid's `homework_kids` row and so everything that cascades from it (assignments, steps, sessions, points, rewards, asks, tutor links, AI run records, the homework-time links). The homework-time events stay on the calendar. Answers the counts. Anthropic's copy of what was sent (if any) can't be reached from here, and the page says so.

**My day: Jordan** (SECURITY DEFINER; revoke from public, service_role; grant to anon, authenticated, as 0002's token functions). Each starts `me := private.homework_kid_by_token(p_token); if me.id is null then return null; end if;`: an unknown, malformed or replaced token, an inactive person, someone who isn't a kid, a kid whose homework is off, and a body of `{}` all get null, which `day.js` reads as "this link no longer works" for these views. Each writer then calls `private.count_homework(me.id, 'writes', 150)` (a Start counts as `'starts'`, 20 a day), calls `private.homework_trim(me.id)`, writes as "Jordan (My day)" (the person's own short name), and answers `{…} || private.homework_view_kid(me.id, me.language)`, so the page redraws without a second read. Text over its limit is refused with 22023 before anything is counted. A write that finds its own earlier work (a resend) changes nothing, isn't counted, and answers the view.
- `homework_by_token(p_token text) returns jsonb` (STABLE): his view (§3.4).
- `step_by_token(p_token text, p_step uuid, p_action text, p_text text default null, p_day date default null, p_key uuid default null) returns jsonb` → `private.step_act(…, 'kid', …)`: `'done'` (answers `paid`: a milestone's first half, §8.1; 0 for a plain step or a half already paid), `'undo'`, `'not_today'` (`p_day` required: the step's day as his screen showed it; a step already moved from it is left as it is), `'ask'` (`p_text` ≤ 200), `'smaller'` (`p_text` 3 to 80; `p_key` required, the new step's id) (§4.5). A step of another kid, of a plan not given to him, or unknown → 42501 "That step isn't on your plan.", the same answer for each.
- `plan_by_token(p_token text, p_assignment uuid, p_steps jsonb, p_rev int) returns jsonb` → `private.plan_save(…, 'kid', …)` with his rules (§4.3). `p_steps` null is **Re-plan** (§4.5).
- `focus_by_token(p_token text, p_action text, p_session uuid, p_minutes int default null, p_step uuid default null, p_assignment uuid default null, p_with text default null, p_result text default null, p_note text default null) returns jsonb` → `private.focus_act`: `'start'`, `'switch'`, `'end'` (`p_result` `'done'`, `'more'`, `'stopped'`, or `'time'` when the ring ran out; §7.1).
- `reward_by_token(p_token text, p_action text, p_reward uuid default null, p_text text default null, p_key uuid default null, p_cost int default null) returns jsonb`: `'ask'`, `'cash'` (an instant reward, on the spot; `p_cost` the price his button showed, §8.5) and `'suggest'` (`p_key` required: the new row's id), `'cancel'` (§8.5); another action → 22023 "Choose ask, cash, cancel or suggest." While points are off, each action answers 42501 "Points are off."
- `assignment_by_token(p_token text, p_action text, p_assignment uuid, p_title text default null, p_subject text default null, p_due_date date default null, p_minutes int default null) returns jsonb` (§4.1):
  - `'add'`: homework he writes down himself, with `p_assignment` the id his phone made. Title 1–120, class ≤ 40, a due date from today to today + 60; no teacher's text (that stays with the adults); `added_by` `'kid'`. At most 15 open ones he added. With `p_minutes` it also does `'quick'`, unless he has no homework time at all: then the add alone, with no plan (`'quick'` itself still gets 22023 "Set a homework time first.").
  - `'quick'`: on an open assignment of his with no plan (his own or an adult's) due within 3 days, one plain step of `p_minutes` (5–60), titled as the assignment, placed by §5.4 (the first homework day with room, so tonight when there is room) and given at once (`given_by` "Jordan (My day)", shown as `'kid'`). Due 4 or more days out, or already planned → 42501 "Plan this one with Pat."
  - `'drop'`: only one he added, with no done step.
- `homework_prefs_by_token(p_token text, p_cue text, p_if_stuck text, p_minutes int) returns jsonb`: his start cue (≤ 120), his if-stuck line (≤ 120) and his first length (10, 15, 20 or 25). Null clears a line.

**Pat's link** (the same grants). Each starts `l := private.tutor_link_by_token(p_token); if l.id is null then return null; end if;` (an unknown, malformed or replaced token, a link that is off or past its end date, or a kid whose homework is off or who is no longer active: null). The writers then call `private.count_homework(l.id, 'writes', 200)` and `private.homework_trim(l.kid_id)`, and write as `<label> (tutor link)`.
- `tutor_view_by_token(p_token text, p_assignment uuid default null, p_part text default 'all') returns jsonb`: Pat's view (§3.4); `p_part = 'ai'` answers only the assignment's AI state, for the poll while a run is open. It records `last_used_at` (to the minute, without touching `updated_at`), so it isn't STABLE.
- `tutor_assignment_by_token(p_token text, p_fields jsonb) returns jsonb`: the page always sends an `id`. An id not yet used adds an assignment or lesson with that id for the link's kid (`added_by` `'tutor'`); one of that kid's changes it; any other → 42501 "That assignment isn't on this link." Fields `kind, title, subject, due_date, details, stretch, status` (`'open'`, `'done'`, `'dropped'`; never a delete), and `ai` (`'undo'` or `'dismiss'`), which does that to the assignment's AI draft as `homework_ai_draft` does (§6.10; only on an assignment that exists). `details` over 4,000 characters, or any field over its limit, → 22023 "That's too long." before any work.
- `tutor_plan_by_token(p_token text, p_assignment uuid, p_steps jsonb, p_rev int, p_give boolean default false) returns jsonb` → `private.plan_save(…, 'tutor', …)`.
- `tutor_step_by_token(p_token text, p_step uuid, p_action text, p_text text default null) returns jsonb` → `private.step_act(…, 'tutor', …)`: `'check'` (optional note), `'not_yet'` (note required), `'uncheck'`, `'answer'` (its own answer can be changed for 15 minutes).

**Jobs** (SECURITY DEFINER; revoke from public, anon, authenticated; grant to service_role, as `calendar_begin`):
