- **The next-reward bar**, always on his homework card, `#homework`, `#points` and the timer screen (while the ring runs, at its end, after his answer and on the break; `plan.goalBar`, from the view's `goal`): "6 points to A new book" toward the cheapest reward he can't afford yet and hasn't asked for (an ask's points are held already), with a bar of his free points against its cost; or "You can get 15 minutes of extra game time now!" when one is his to get (the dearest he can cash or ask for), with the way to the next under it.
- **Instant rewards** (`reward_by_token(…, 'cash', p_reward)`, with `p_key` and `p_cost`): **Get it now**, then **Yes, get it · −15**, drawn where **Get it now** was and taking a tap only a moment later (the second tap of a double tap spends nothing). `p_cost` is the price his button showed: nobody sees a cash-in first, so a price a parent changed since (or none) is refused, 22023 "That reward's price changed.", and his screen reads the reward again. A cash-in's `p_key` is kept until an answer comes (or a read shows it landed), through **Cancel** and other views, so a tap again after a lost answer finds the cash-in it made; a refused or unanswered one reads his view again. An active, priced, instant reward of his whose cost his free points (balance less what his waiting asks hold) cover (else 42501 "Not enough points yet."), and fewer than `homework_kids.instant_day` (2, a parent sets 0 to 10) cashed today (else P0001 "That's all the instant rewards for today. Ask a parent for this one."). The points go at once: an ask row with status `'cashed'`, decided at once by him, and a `'trade'` entry of −cost. A resent cash (the same `p_key`) finds its row; a reward that isn't instant → 42501 "Ask a parent for that one." His screen says "Got it: <reward>. Tell a parent."; the parents see "Jordan cashed in: <reward> · 15 · 4:52pm" on Today that day, and "(instant, cashed in by Jordan)" in Recent. A mis-tap is put right with **Give points**.
- **Ask** (`reward_by_token(…, 'ask', p_reward)`, with `p_key`): an active, priced reward of his (an instant one too, when today's are used up); `available` = balance − points held by his waiting asks must be at least its cost (else 42501 "Not enough points yet."); at most 3 waiting. The points are held. A resent ask finds its own row. His screen then says "Tell a parent you asked."
- **Cancel:** his own waiting ask.
- **Suggest:** a title of at most 60 characters; it arrives with no price and off (`suggested`), at most 5 waiting; a parent prices it (it becomes active) or deletes it.
- **A parent decides** on Today or the Homework page (`decide_reward`): **Yes** checks the balance again and writes a `'trade'` entry of −cost; **Not now** takes nothing and can carry a word he sees ("Not on a school night"). A mis-tapped Yes is put right with **Give points**.
- He sees his asks under **Waiting for a parent**, and decided ones (and the instant ones he got, "Got it") for 7 days.

### 8.6 Tone

The words are a project manager's: "started on time", "block finished", "milestone checked". The one celebration is small and quick, when points arrive: his balance counts up, a "+30" shows beside it, and a short two-note sound plays if sound is on. No mascots, applause, streak flames or confetti. Feedback is about what he did, against his own past: "Outline checked by Pat", "45 minutes focused today", "finished 2 days early".

## 9. Pages

### 9.1 Homework (`#/homework`, `#/homework/<assignment id>`), signed in

`HomeHub.register({ name: 'homework', schema: { min: 5, max: 99 }, nav: { label: 'Homework', order: 37 }, match, render })`. The heading follows the house pattern: `<h1>Homework</h1>`, with the meta line "Plans, focus sessions and points".

```
Homework                                                            [Jordan ▾]   (only with two kids)
Jordan · 318 points · Homework time Mon–Thu 4:30–5:15, Sun 5:00–5:45 ›
TO APPROVE    Pick the movie · 200 · asked Sun · balance 318 (this week +150 focus, +80 milestones)  [Yes]  [Not now]
              Suggested by Jordan: "Skip one chore" · Price it ›
TO CHECK      ◆ Outline with 3 main points · Science project · ticked Wed       [Looks done]  [Not yet]
TO TALK ABOUT Science project · "Full draft" was planned for Mon · Re-plan didn't reach it
QUESTIONS AND NOTES
              Worksheet 4.2 · "What does 'simplify fully' mean here?"           [Answer]
              Science project · Answered by Pat · Tue 6:10pm: "Yes, a short title at the top."
              Science project · Pat's note on the outline · Thu: "Clear outline. The third point is the strongest."
ASSIGNMENTS AND LESSONS                                              [+ Assignment]  [+ Lesson]
  Science project: river poster · Science · due Fri Nov 13           4 of 9 · Given by Pat Lee, tutor
     Work left ██████░░░░ 1 h 40       Homework time left ██████████ 3 h 15
  Math · Worksheet 5.1 · due Wed · added by Jordan                    Quick · 15 min
  Fractions practice · lesson · do by Fri Nov 6                      No plan yet  [Plan it ›]
  DONE (30 DAYS) ›
THIS WEEK     Mon 45 free · Tue 30 (Piano 5:00) · Wed 45 · Thu 20 over · Fri — · Sat — · Sun 45
              Focused 1 h 40 (last week 1 h 15) · Steps take about 1.3× the planned time
              Mon 4:31 · 20 min · Step done · "found 2 sources" ›
POINTS AND REWARDS   318 · today +30 of 60 · milestones this week 40 of 160   [Give points]  [Edit rewards]
              Recent: −15 Extra game time (instant, cashed in by Jordan), Wed · +20 Checked: Outline, Wed ·
                      +20 Ticked: Outline, Tue · +20 Focus, Tue · +10 On time, Tue                       [Void…]
SWITCHES      (§9.2)
```

- **First visit:** "Turn on homework for Jordan" (a list of kids; parents), then the homework-time form, prefilled Mon–Thu 4:30–5:15, then "Add 3 rewards" in three sizes, the first one instant (§8.5).
- **Rewards** (**+ Reward** and each reward's edit): title, points, on, and "Instant: he gets it on the spot, no ask (you see it on Today; at most 2 a day)"; the list marks the instant ones "· instant". Recent says "(instant, cashed in by Jordan)" on the trade of one he got himself, and "Ticked: <milestone>" on a milestone's first half.
- **An assignment** (`#/homework/<id>`): title, kind, class, due date, who added it, the teacher's text (a large box; pasted text keeps its lines, with the Live Text hint), the stretch question, status; below it the plan editor (§9.4), the AI part (§6.10, PR B), its steps' questions, answers and notes with who and when (`qa_log` included), and its sessions (day, minutes, with whom, result, his note).
- **Sections in this order:** To approve · To check · To talk about · Questions and notes · Assignments and lessons · This week · Points and rewards · Switches. A section with nothing in it is left out. **Questions and notes** lists open questions with **Answer**, and every answer and coach's note of the last 7 days with who gave it, so the parents see everything Pat writes to Jordan.
- **Reads:** `.from('homework_kids' | 'homework_times' | 'tutor_links' | 'assignments' | 'homework_steps' | 'focus_sessions' | 'points_ledger' | 'rewards' | 'reward_asks' | 'ai_runs')`, `.from('household')`, and `.rpc('homework_week', …)`. On open, on focus and after each write; every 5 seconds only while an AI run is open on the assignment shown, until its lease ends (one `assignments` row). No other polling.
- **Writes:** the column grants (§3.2) and the functions in §3.5. Settings are saved with the `updated_at` check, as Food's switches are. The AI goes through `functions.invoke('hub-jobs', {body: {action: 'homework_ai', …}})` with the parent's sign-in (PR B).
- **Pat's link** is sent as the Family page sends My day links: Copy · WhatsApp · Text, with "Hi Pat, here is Jordan's homework page: <site>/day.html?h=<token>. It shows only his homework. Please open it on your own phone or computer." in the link's language.
- **Sam** sees it all read-only; the buttons aren't drawn for him, and the database refuses anyway.
- On schema 3 and 4 the module isn't active: no tab, no route, no request.

### 9.2 The switches card

One line per part: what it does, what it costs, what off stops.

| Part | Line | Off stops | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Homework for Jordan** | "Homework on Jordan's My day, and Pat's link." | his card and views; the link answers "no longer works"; the AI refuses; nothing is deleted | off until a parent turns it on |
| **Points** | "Points for on-time starts, focus blocks (paid the moment a block ends) and milestones (half when he ticks one, half when it's checked)." Values (per focused minute, starting on time, a milestone), the daily cap, the weekly milestone cap, the instant rewards he may get a day, and the focus length offered first, editable. | earning; points on his screen | on |
| **Pat Lee, tutor** (each link) | label, language, "Ends Apr 30 · Last used Tue 4:05pm"; Copy · WhatsApp · Text · **New link** · **Off** · **Extend 180 days** · Can ask the AI (PR B) | that link, at once | on when a parent makes it |
| **AI planner** (PR B) | "When on, Pat and the parents can send an assignment (its title, class, dates and text), Jordan's free minutes per day, how long his steps take against the plan, and his short answers to the AI's questions, with the names Home Hub knows taken out, to Anthropic (Claude) to draft a plan. **What Claude will see** shows exactly what goes. Anthropic doesn't train on it. It may keep it up to 30 days, or up to 2 years if its safety checks flag it. About 6 cents a plan; never more than the $15 monthly limit." The key's state (from the last run), this month's runs and cost, **Test the AI planner**, **Words never to send**. The switch is drawn only after a Test answered "Working", and the database refuses it with no never-send word ("Add the school's name first"). Spanish to match. | every AI request; starter plans still work | **off** |
| **Erase Jordan's homework** | type ERASE; says what it deletes, and that Anthropic's copy (if any) can't be reached from here | — | — |
| (0006) **School links** | §12 | — | none pasted |

### 9.3 Today

When `core.schemaVersion >= 5` and the signed-in person is an admin, Today reads waiting `reward_asks`, the instant rewards cashed today (`reward_asks` with status `'cashed'`), done and unchecked milestones and unanswered questions (`homework_steps`, one request), and this week's ledger rows for the ask's kid. One line each at the top, only when there is something:
- "Jordan asks: Pick the movie · 200 · balance 318 (this week: +150 focus and on time, +40 milestones ticked, +40 milestones checked by Pat)   Yes · Not now" (one tap each, `decide_reward`);
- "Jordan cashed in: 15 minutes of extra game time · 15 · 4:52pm" (an instant reward; no button: nothing to decide);
- "Homework: 1 question · 2 milestones to check ›" (to the Homework page).

No line and no request for Sam or below 5. If the read fails, the lines are left out and the day draws as usual.

### 9.4 The plan editor (`assets/plan.js`)

The same editor on the Homework page, on Pat's link and on Jordan's `#plan/<id>`, with each one's rules (§4.3). It draws into an element it is given and makes no database calls: each page hands it an `api` (`save`, `step`, and from PR B `ai` and `preview`).

```
Science project: river poster · due Fri Nov 13 (11 days)
Teacher's text ▸                                                       What Claude will see ▾
[ Send to Claude ]   [ Start from a starter plan ]   [ Add a step ]
DRAFT · Suggested by AI (Claude). Check it with Jordan.          [Use this plan]  [Undo]  [Dismiss]
 Mon 2   ▢ Copy the requirements into your notes · 10          start          AI   ⋯
 Tue 3   ▢ Find 2 sources and save their links · 20                           AI   ⋯
 …
 Wed 4   ◆ Outline with 3 main points · 25                     milestone
 Wed 11  ▢ Check it against the instructions · 20               check
 Fri 13  ▢ Turn it in · 5                                       turn in
 Work left ██████░░ 3 h 20    Homework time left █████████ 5 h 10 · Fits · 1 day spare
 Questions: Did your teacher say which materials are allowed?   [answer]
 Optional: Which part of the river would you most like to show in detail?
 Ask for changes: (Smaller steps) (Fewer steps) (Nothing on Wed) (Start later) (More time for writing) [note…]
 [ Save ]   [ Give Jordan this plan ]                      (a given plan: "On Jordan's My day since Mon · by Pat")
```

- Each row: a box (done), ◆ for a milestone, title, minutes, day (a picker of the days up to the due date, homework days first, "not a homework day" marked), **⋯** (title, done looks like, minutes, milestone on or off, move up, move down, delete). Done steps are read-only. Buttons, not drag.
- **Place again** sends the steps not done without days (after a due date moves, or a week went sideways); for Jordan it is **Re-plan** (§4.5).
- While an AI run is open: "Thinking about a plan… (usually under a minute)"; the editor stays usable.
- A conflict (`plan_rev`) reloads with "Changed since you opened it: here it is now."
- Before PR B the AI parts (the preview, **Send to Claude**, the draft, Ask for changes) aren't drawn.

### 9.5 Pat's link (`day.html?h=<token>`)

A third mode of `day.html`, beside the day view and the confirmation-only page. `?h=` with no `t=` loads nobody's day: `day.js` hands over to `plan.js`'s tutor view, giving it an `api` that calls only the four `tutor_*_by_token` functions and, from PR B, `functions.invoke('hub-jobs')` for the AI (the token in the body, never the URL of the call). Words in the link's language. A null answer → "This link no longer works. Ask Jordan's family for a new one." Before 0005: "This link doesn't work yet."

```
Homework · Jordan                                      Hi Pat Lee, tutor · Link ends Apr 30
Start with what he did. Ask what's next and when he'll start it.
TO CHECK      ◆ Outline with 3 main points · Science project · ticked Wed   [Looks done]  [Not yet]
TO TALK ABOUT Science project · "Full draft" was planned for Mon · Ask what got in the way, then pick a new day together.
QUESTIONS     Worksheet 4.2 · "What does 'simplify fully' mean here?"       [Answer]
PLANS TO LOOK AT   Fractions practice · AI draft ready ›
ASSIGNMENTS   [+ Assignment] [+ Lesson]
  Science project: river poster · due Fri Nov 13       4 of 9 · Full draft Mon
  Math · Worksheet 5.1 · due Wed · added by Jordan     1 of 1
  Fractions practice · lesson · do by Fri Nov 6        No plan yet ›
HIS WEEK      Mon 45 free · Tue 30 · Wed 45 · Thu 20 over · Fri — · Sat — · Sun 45   (busy: no titles)
LAST 14 DAYS  Mon 20 min · Step done · "found 2 sources" · Tue 25 min with you · More to do …
              Steps take about 1.3× the planned time
```

- **The order is fixed** (To check, To talk about, Questions, Plans to look at, Assignments, His week), so the check-in at each session takes under 15 minutes: look at what he did, ask rather than tell, answer, then plan the next piece. The first line, in the link's language, says so.
- **Looks done** opens a short optional note ("say what's good about it"); **Not yet** needs one ("say what's missing"). Both show on his screen and to the parents (§4.5).
- An assignment opens the editor (§9.4), with the teacher's text, **Send to Claude** (PR B; when the AI is on and the link may ask), the starter plans, and **Give Jordan this plan**.
- No points, rewards, asks, events by name (with the coach view on, the Coach section shows his own, §20.6), names other than Jordan's first name and the link's own label, and no "by Alex": who did something is "Jordan", "you" or "the family". **Nothing live:** no "focusing now", no block still running, and no "alone"; a finished block says "with you" only when it was marked With Pat.
- Read on open, on `focus` and `visibilitychange`, and, while an AI run is open, `tutor_view_by_token(…, p_part => 'ai')` every 5 seconds until the lease ends. Nothing is saved on the device but the language and the preview's hash.

### 9.6 Jordan's My day

`day.js` keeps its rules: dates and times from the database only, one request at a time, no redraw when nothing changed, every fixed word in English and Spanish, and the 5-minute refresh redraws only the day view (so it never touches a running ring). `viewFromHash`, `render`, the hashchange handler, `refreshSoon` and the stop functions each get a branch. Every view also works at a computer's width and in a 420px-wide window (§7.2).

**The card** (under the greeting, above today's items, only when `get_person_day` has `homework`):
```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Homework · 4:30 · 3 steps · 55 min · 2 from earlier    ★ 118 │
│ Next: Science project · Find 2 sources   [ Start · 10 ]  Open › │
│ 82 points to A new book  ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░ │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```
The last line is the next-reward bar (§8.5), on the card, `#homework`, `#points` and `#focus`.
**Start** on the card starts a block on Next at his first length (the tap is the user gesture for Wake Lock and the `AudioContext`, as on `#homework`) and opens `#focus`; one tap from My day to a running ring. While a block runs: "Focus · 12 min left ›". Without the key (anyone else, homework off, or schema below 5) there is no card and **no homework request**.

**`#homework`** (from `homework_by_token`; on open, on `focus` and `visibilitychange`, every 60 seconds while visible; never while `#focus` is on screen):
```
‹ My day                                               Homework            ★ 118
82 points to A new book  ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░
NOW · homework time 4:30–5:15 · start by 4:40 for +10
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Find 2 sources and save their titles and links in your notes │
│ Science project · step 3 of 9 · 20 min                       │
│ Done looks like: 2 titles and links in your notes doc        │
│ "When I've had my snack, I open my notes doc."  ✎            │
│  ((10)) (15) (20) (25)                          Alone ▾      │
│ [                   Start · 10 min                         ] │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
TONIGHT · 55 min planned · 45 min of homework time
 ▢ Science project · Find 2 sources · 20
 ▢ Math · Worksheet 4.2 · 15                           Pat: "Simplify means…"
 Math · Worksheet 5.1 · due Wed                        [Quick] [Start]
 FROM EARLIER · 2 steps · 30 min · Re-plan ›
 + Homework
DUE SOON
 Science project · due Fri Nov 13 · ◆ Outline Wed
   Work left ████░░░░ 1 h 40 · Homework time left ████████ 3 h 15
   Optional: Which part of the river would you most like to show in detail?
 Spanish · Vocabulary project · due Mon · Behind the plan · Re-plan ›
 Fractions practice · lesson · do by Fri Nov 6 · being planned
Steps take about 1.3× the planned time · 1 h 40 focused this week (last week 1 h 15)
Pat and the grown-ups in your family see your plan and your notes. The grown-ups see your points.
```
- **Now** is the card's Next: the first open step of Tonight, or the first earlier one when tonight has none; tapping any other step makes it Now. His start cue (his words, set once with ✎, `homework_prefs_by_token`) is on it. "Alone ▾" can be **With Pat** or **With a parent**.
- Tapping a step's box ticks it (toast "Done · Undo" for 15 minutes; a milestone's toast says "+20 now · +20 more when Pat checks it", and his balance counts up). ⋯ on a step: Done, Not today, Make it smaller, Ask Pat, Edit (opens `#plan/<id>`).
- An assignment with no plan: "Math · Worksheet 5.1 · due Wed · [Quick] [Start]" (§4.1). **+ Homework** opens a short form: what, class, due, and "about how many minutes?" (prefilled 15).
- **From earlier** is folded: one line, opening to its steps, each with **Start** and **Not today**, and **Re-plan** for all of them (§4.5). **Behind the plan** is plain text, never red.
- A milestone checked with a note, or sent back with **Not yet**, shows the note under it: "Pat: Clear outline. The third point is the strongest." / "Not yet: Add a source for the second point."

**`#focus`** (the ring, §7.2): the step and its "done looks like" line, his start cue for the first minute; "On time · +10 once 5 minutes are done"; the live count, "★ 118 +12 so far", and the next-reward bar; **I'm stuck**, **Done** (ticks the step, then **Keep going: <next step>** or **End the block**), **Stop**.

**The stuck sheet** (**I'm stuck**; the ring keeps running underneath):
```
Stuck? Your plan: "If I'm stuck for 5 minutes, I'll write the question for Pat and move on."
 [ Make it smaller ]   a tiny first piece: Write one sentence · List what's unclear · Do the easiest part · or your own
 [ Ask Pat ]           a short question Pat sees; this step stays open
 [ Next step ]         Math · Worksheet 4.2
 [ Back to the timer ]
```
No AI here. With no if-stuck line yet, the first line says "Write your own if-stuck plan ✎". **Make it smaller** moves the running block to the new step.

**The end screen** (the ring is empty, or **End the block** or **Stop** was tapped). The ring running out ends the block at once (End `'time'`, §7.1), so the points are there before he answers, with the small celebration (§7.2):
```
Time. How did it go?
 +20 · 20 minutes · +10 on time                           ★ 148 +30
 What did you get done? (optional)  [ found 2 sources, 1 more to check ]
 [ Step done ✓ ]          [ More to do ]
```
and after his answer:
```
Block finished.
 +20 · 20 minutes · +10 on time                                 ★ 148
 [ Break · 5 min ]                                [ Next: Worksheet 4.2 › ]
```
The points line counts what the server saw ("+12 · 12 minutes"); under a minute it says "Under a minute: no points this time." Over the daily cap: "Today's limit reached."

**`#points`** (when points are on): the balance and "+30 today" (no daily target: the cap appears only as "Today's limit reached" when it is hit), and the next-reward bar; **Waiting for a parent** (his asks, with Cancel, "105 free to trade" while they wait, and "Tell a parent you asked."); **Rewards** ("15 minutes of extra game time · 15 · instant" with **Get it now** → **Yes, get it · −15** · Cancel; the others Ask, or "40 more"; "No more instant rewards today: ask a parent." once today's are used); **Suggest a reward** ("a parent prices it"); **Decided** ("Yes", "Not now", "Got it" for an instant one); the last 10 entries ("+20 Focus · Tue", "+20 Milestone done · Wed", "Void: Block left running while away · Wed").

**`#plan/<id>`:** the editor (§9.4) with his rules (§4.3) and **Re-plan**; milestones and the turn-in shown with a lock: "Milestones are checkpoints you set with Pat. Change them together."

"Pat" in these words is `me.tutor` (the first word of the tutor link's label), filled in by the page; with no tutor link, "Ask Pat" becomes "Ask for help" ("Pedir ayuda"), which a parent answers. `friendly()` (`day.js`) learns the new database messages **before** its `42501` line, as v1.2 did: "That step isn't on your plan", "Only your own tick", "That's a checkpoint", "Finish the block you started first", "That block isn't yours", "That's a lot for one day", "No homework day before it's due has room", "was changed since you opened it", "Not enough points yet", "Points are off", "Choose 10, 15, 20 or 25", "Plan this one with Pat", "Set a homework time first", "Those points are already spent", "Ask a parent for that one", "That's all the instant rewards for today". Each gets a Spanish `day.test.js` case. Step and assignment titles stay as typed; the starter plans' titles are in the language of whoever made the plan, and can be renamed.

### 9.8 Who sees and taps what

| Who | Sees | Taps |
|---|---|---|
| Alex, Morgan (parents) | the Homework page, all of it, with every question, answer and note Pat or they wrote to Jordan; the Today lines; the homework-time event | everything: plans, homework time, rewards, Yes and Not now, Looks done and Not yet with a note, Answer and clear, Pat's link, the AI and its switch, points, Erase |
| Sam (adult) | the Homework page, read-only; the homework-time event | nothing new |
| Jordan (kid) | his card, `#homework`, `#focus`, `#points`, `#plan/<id>`; his homework-time event; Pat's and his parents' notes on his milestones | + Homework, Quick, tick, Undo (15 minutes), Not today, Re-plan, Make it smaller, Ask Pat, Start (on the card too), Keep going, Next step, the end buttons, his note, his cue and if-stuck line, Ask and Cancel, Suggest; reorder and move his plain steps |
| Pat (link) | his assignments with the teacher's text, plans and drafts, questions, milestones to check and to talk about, homework time with "Busy", his finished sessions ("with you" or not) and notes, his pace | add and edit assignments and lessons, plan, Send to Claude, Give Jordan this plan, Looks done and Not yet with a note, Answer |
| Taylor (helper) | the homework-time event, if she's along on it | nothing new |
| Anyone at the wall | the homework-time event, as any event ("Private item" if ticked) | nothing |

## 10. Notifications

| Where | v1.3 | 0006 | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| Jordan's My day | the card; Pat's answers, checks and notes and the parents' decisions appear on his next look | "New from school" | his channel |
| Today (parents) | one line per ask (with his balance and this week's sources), and one for questions and milestones to check, only when there is something | — | §9.3 |
| Texts ("Send Jordan his day") | the homework-time event, as today | one homework line (§12) | `day_text` reaches it |
| Morning email | the parents' block for Jordan shows the homework-time event, as today | the homework line, through `day_text` | never through `digest_for`, so no wrapper rule |
| The wall | the homework-time event only, as any event | — | the wall is public in the house |
| Pat | nothing pushed; Pat looks at the link before a session | — | no Pat person, no email |
| Jordan's reminder to start | a repeating alarm on his phone, set by the family, and optionally a Shortcuts automation that opens his homework screen when he stops it (README) | — | a web page can't ring |
| Push or SMS | none | none | not in the hub (v2-design "Later") |

**Never** in an email, a text or on the wall: the teacher's text, step titles in a subject, questions, notes, points, rewards, asks, AI drafts, the school link; never a link that changes something.

## 11. Blackbaud: what a family can get, and the import

From the research note (Blackbaud's help pages read on 2026-10-30), and from what the owner saw on the family's own school site (2026-10-31, on a parent's login), marked **confirmed**. What is still not known is marked "not confirmed".

### 11.1 What a family can get

- **Confirmed: a parent's own login is enough.** On a parent's login the school's Blackbaud site (myschoolapp) shows a **"Jordan's Calendars"** section (his name). Its **Calendar** page has a feed button that opens **"WebCal Feed for External Calendar"**, with **Entire Calendar** and **Individual Filter Feeds**: **Assignments**, **Schedule**, **Games/Practices**, **Class Events** and **Group Events**. The parent copies each link with right-click → **Copy Link Address**. No student login is needed. (The research note's worry, that Blackbaud's help lists no Assignments calendar for parents and the route might need Jordan's login, is settled.)
- **Confirmed: the window and the lag.** The box says the feed holds events for the **past 2 months and the upcoming 12 months**, and that changes to the settings can take **up to an hour** to show in it.
- **What 0006 uses:** **Assignments** (his assignments, which become New from school), and **Schedule** and **Games/Practices** (his classes and games, as busy time for placement). Class Events and Group Events aren't used: they are the school's events, not his time.
- **The link:** `webcal://<school>.myschoolapp.com/podium/feed/iCal.aspx?z=<…>` (or `https://`). The `z=` value is the only thing that identifies him: **anyone with the link can read his calendar**, and no way to cancel or reset a link was found. So it is kept like a password (§11.3).
- **What's in an item:** the title and the dates are certain. The class is probably in the title ("Biology - 2 (B): Lab report"). Whether an assignment's dates are its due date or a span from the assigned date to the due date, the exact all-day form, the time zone and how stable the UIDs are, are **not confirmed**: the reader takes every form the research warned about (§11.3), and the first-import check (§11.2, step 7) sets the date rule. The description, when there is one, is kept as the teacher's text (§11.3); attachments almost certainly aren't there, so a longer sheet is still pasted.
- **Two school switches** decide whether the Assignments feed has anything in it: the platform manager allows iCal export, and an academic admin ticks **"iCal-Assignments"** for the year and school level (KB 23740). Whether that must be turned on again each school year is not confirmed; an empty read after one with items says so (§11.3).
- **What a family can't get:** the SKY API (Blackbaud's real API) needs a school admin to connect an app; there is no family CSV export; scraping the site would mean storing a password and is not done. Per-assignment notification emails exist but are off by default, per teacher, with no digest: Later at most.

### 11.2 The owner's steps (about 5 minutes, on a computer; 0006)

Labels vary a little by school; where they differ, the step says what to look for.

**A. Get the links (on your own parent login)**
1. Open the school's Blackbaud site (`https://<school>.myschoolapp.com`) and sign in **as yourself**.
2. Under **Jordan's Calendars**, open **Calendar**.
3. Click the feed button: **WebCal Feed for External Calendar** opens.
4. Under **Individual Filter Feeds**, right-click **Assignments** → **Copy Link Address**. (Not **Entire Calendar**: it mixes everything.) It starts with `webcal://` or `https://` and contains `/podium/feed/iCal.aspx?z=`.
5. Optional, same box: **Schedule** and **Games/Practices** (busy time only), one at a time.

**B. Give them to Home Hub**
6. Home Hub → **Homework → Switches → School links → Assignments** → paste → **Save** (and the same for Schedule and Games/Practices). The hub shows only "…a1b2" from then on, and never the link again.
7. **Check now** (or wait for the next hourly read, at 23 minutes past). The card shows the next 5 items: "Biology · Lab report · due Thu Nov 19 — is this what Blackbaud shows?" When fewer than 5 are still to come (a weekend, a holiday: the link holds only what was due in the last 14 days and later), the latest past ones fill it, marked "was due Fri Nov 20", and the card says so: "Nothing is due yet, so these are the latest ones." (or "Only 2 are still to come, so the latest past ones fill in.") "Are these the dates Blackbaud shows? It still lists past assignments." (0008; before it, the card had nothing to ask until something new was posted). An empty check says why, by the link's status: not read yet ("Once the Assignments link is read, its next 5 items show here…"; from 0008 also for a link removed and added again, until its first read); read with nothing in it ("…was read today 3:10pm and held no assignments due from 2 weeks ago on. The next ones show here once the school posts some…"); or, on a database before 0008, read with every item past ("…all 18 of its assignments are already past. The next ones show here once the school posts one due today or later…"). For a read link that Home Hub isn't reading, the end says what to do first: paused, "It is paused, and Home Hub doesn't read it until you resume it; then the next ones show here…"; its last read stopped on something only **Replace** mends (the stop's own words say "use Replace": a bad link, a redirect, a refused or unknown link, a sign-in page, not a calendar), "Its last read didn't work: copy the link again and use Replace; then the next ones show here…". The toast after an answer says what his New from school holds after it: "…shows them now." when something is on it (an item due yesterday is due today after **A day later**), else "…shows the next ones once the school posts them." Tap **Looks right**, **Use the first date** (an item's dates are a span from the assigned day), or **One day off**. Until one is tapped, nothing from the Assignments link reaches Jordan or Pat; the Schedule and Games links are busy time at once.

**C. If step 3 or 7 fails** (no feed button; no Assignments row; "0 assignments"; an error), send the school one email:

> Hello, we'd like to add our son's assignments calendar to our family calendar. In Blackbaud, could you please turn on calendar feeds for families, and tick "iCal-Assignments" for his grade this year (Academics → Content → Class Pages → Class Page Options → Classes)? Blackbaud's help article is KB 23740. Thank you.

Until they do, typed assignments go on working.

### 11.3 How 0006 reads it

- **Storage:** the address goes into `private.school_feed_urls`, on which no API role has any privilege (not anon, not authenticated, not `service_role`); only `school_begin`, a definer function that only `service_role` may run, reads it, to hand it to `hub-jobs`. Parents write it (`set_school_feed`) and nobody reads it back: not the page, not Pat, not Jordan. Pages see `school_feeds` (the host, the last 4 characters, the status) and `school_feed_status`, never the address.
- **The address check** (`set_school_feed`'s `private.school_url`, and again in `school.ts` before every read): `webcal://` becomes `https://`; https only; port 443; no user info; the host ends in `.myschoolapp.com` (`private.school_host_suffix()` and `SCHOOL_HOST.suffix`, which the tests replace with `.school.example`); the path is `/podium/feed/iCal.aspx`; a `z` value is present; at most 500 characters, with no spaces, quotes or angle brackets. A school on its own domain would need the check widened on purpose (§17.2).
- **The read:** a `school` action in `hub-jobs` (`school.ts`, shaped like `calendar.ts`: lease, read the links that are due within a time budget, store, finish), every hour at minute 23 from a fifth `cron.sql` job, `hub-school`, and on a parent's **Check now** (every link not read in the last minute). No read starts after 30 seconds; each has 15 seconds in all (redirects and body included) and `redirect: 'manual'`; each redirect's `Location` passes the same address check (https, port 443, no user info, the host suffix; any path), at most 3 redirects. At most 3 MB read. Every `VEVENT` is parsed, then only what the hub uses is kept: assignments due from today − 14 days to today + 120 days, schedule and games items from today to today + 55 days (busy time can't use more, §5.3). The caps apply after that filter: 400 assignments and 2,000 schedule or games items per link; over a cap the stop is `too_many`, and nothing is applied. A link the job would read (not paused, his homework on) due and not read for over 2 hours is **stale** on the status card ("run supabase/cron.sql again"); one that can be read again (**Resume**, homework turned back on) is due at once, so it isn't stale straight away. After failures, back off (1, 2, 4, then 8 hours). Links reading well are handed over first. A run stopped partway (the Edge Function's limits) never reaches `school_finish`: `school_sync.handed` holds the links it was handed and hasn't stored, in order, so the next `school_begin` makes the first of them, the one it was reading, a failed read (`error`, backed off), and the others are read; the ones after it were never tried and count nothing. When `school_store` refuses a link's items (an error from PostgREST or the database), `school.ts` stores that read again as `error`, with none, so the link backs off and the card says so.
- **Nothing about the link is ever logged.** Deno's `fetch` error text quotes the whole URL, `z=` included, so every fetch failure becomes a fixed code (`unreachable`, `timeout`, …), as `google.ts` does; `message(err)` (`util.ts`, "never for anything read from a secret") is never used in `school.ts`; nothing there writes to the console; `school_feeds.last_stop` takes only the fixed codes (a check constraint: `bad_link`, `redirect`, `unreachable`, `timeout`, `too_big`, `refused`, `gone`, `down`, `http`, `sign_in`, `not_calendar`, `empty`, `too_many`, `error`), and `school_store` stores any other stop it is sent as `error`. No title is logged either.
- **Parsing** (`ical.ts`, hand-written, no imports, lenient): CRLF, LF or CR; unfolded lines (a space or a tab); `VEVENT` only (a `VALARM` inside one and a `VTIMEZONE` beside them are skipped); `UID`, `SUMMARY`, `DESCRIPTION`, `STATUS`; `DTSTART`/`DTEND` as `VALUE=DATE`, as date-times at midnight (Blackbaud's non-standard all-day form: 00:00 to 00:00, or 00:00 with no end), with `TZID=` (an IANA zone, the usual Windows names such as "Eastern Standard Time", or else the household's zone), `Z` or floating (the household's zone); `DURATION` without `DTEND`; TEXT escapes and HTML entities; a description's HTML made plain text: only what is shaped like a tag goes (a known element's name or a namespaced one such as Word's `o:p`, then up to the next `>` with no `<` on the way; or a comment), so "Graph x < 3 and y > 1" stays as it is. Also:
  - folds are joined on the bytes, before they are decoded (`unfoldBytes` in `ical.ts`), so a fold inside a character's UTF-8 bytes ("Espa" + fold + "ñol") joins it whole (RFC 5545 §3.1);
  - NUL and the other control characters but tab and line breaks, and a lone surrogate, are taken out (Postgres's `jsonb` refuses NUL and a lone surrogate, so one would stop every read of that link); text is cut to what the database keeps by characters, never through the middle of an emoji;
  - every pattern stops at the next `<` or line break it can't use, so a hostile body (tens of KB of `<`, or of blank lines) is read in time in proportion to its size, far inside the Edge Function's CPU limit;
  - an all-day end is exclusive, so it is moved back one day;
  - a cancelled event (`STATUS:CANCELLED`) is left out: one the school took off;
  - repeated keys in one read: the last one wins (`private.school_rows`); the key is the md5 of the UID (for schedule and games, with the start), or without one of the class, the title and the first day (a schedule or games item's title and start), so two classes' "Homework" due the same day are two items;
  - `RRULE` and `RDATE` aren't expanded; the event is read once and the status card counts them (`last_repeats`), so a parent sees it;
  - an HTML page (Blackbaud's sign-in page, for a replaced link) is `sign_in`; anything else without a `VCALENDAR` is `not_calendar`;
  - the class: the part of the title before the first ": ", when it reads like a class ("Biology - 2 (B)" → Biology; not "Chapter 6");
  - **`DESCRIPTION` is kept** (changed from the earlier "isn't kept", P7 in §19; the owner's brief asks for it): cut to 4,000 characters, it is the item's `details` and becomes the assignment's teacher's text, so it goes only where pasted text goes (the parents, Pat, and from PR B the AI after the scrub), never to Jordan's view, never into a log, and is emptied 30 days after the due date like pasted text: the assignment's copy by `homework_trim`, and the item's own and the one in `applied` by every hourly run (`private.school_trim`, from `school_begin`), whatever the link's state (§13.3). A person's own text always wins (below).
- **Never lose anything on one bad or empty read.** An item is "no longer on the school site" (`gone_at`, marked, never deleted) only after two good, non-empty reads in a row without it, while its date is still inside the window (an item that ages out isn't "missing"); a schedule or games item gone is deleted, being only busy time. Without a UID, an item the last good read saw, still inside the window and missing from this one, and a new key of the same class and title in it are one item whose date moved; an older one of the same title (last week's "Reading log", past the window) is another item. A read with 0 items when there were 3 or more is `empty` ("School feed is empty: new school year?") and not applied. A failed read changes nothing but the link's status.
- **Assignments from school.** An Assignments item becomes an assignment (`added_by` `'school'`: "from school") only when Jordan taps **Quick** or **Plan with Pat**, or a parent taps **Add** (§11.4): its title, class, due date and description. From then on the school's changes reach it **a field at a time, and never a field a person changed** (Jordan, Pat, a parent, or the retention trim): the item keeps what the feed last wrote there (`applied`), and a field follows the school only while it still has that value. A due date never moves by itself on an assignment with a plan: Pat's view, his and the parents' status card mark it (`moved_to`, "Due date moved from Fri to Mon · Place again?"), and a person places it again. An item no longer on the school site leaves its assignment as it is, marked (`school.gone`).
- **The first-import check** (§11.2 step 7) sets the date rule (an item's first or last day) and a shift (−1 to 1 day) for that link; until it is answered, its items are visible only to the parents, in the check. From 0008 the check is up to 5 of the link's items still on the school site: the ones to come, earliest first, and when fewer than 5 are to come, the latest past ones to fill it (§12.8).
- **Status** on the switches card (`school_feed_status`): "Assignments link · <school>.myschoolapp.com …a1b2 · last checked 10:23 · 23 assignments", or a fixed message per stop (`sign_in`: "Blackbaud answered with a sign-in page: the link may have been replaced"; `not_calendar`: "isn't a calendar"; `empty`: "came back empty: new school year?"; `gone`: "Blackbaud doesn't know this link any more"; `stale`: "not checked for over 3 hours: run supabase/cron.sql again (README)").

### 11.4 In Jordan's day: New from school (0006)

On his `#homework`, after Due soon (his view's `from_school`, at most 20, due from today to today + 30), and a count on his card (`get_person_day`'s homework `from_school`):
```
NEW FROM SCHOOL (2)
 History · Read chapter 5 · due Wed           [Quick]  [Plan with Pat]  [Hide]
 Biology · Lab report · due Fri Nov 20        [Plan with Pat]  [Hide]
```
- **Quick** (offered for one due within 3 days, `quick: true`): one tap makes it an assignment from school with one step of his guess of the minutes, placed by §5.4 on the first homework day with room, given at once: "this one's easy, skip the plan". It is v1.3's Quick, with its rules: one due 4 or more days out answers "Plan this one with Pat." and nothing is made.
- **Plan with Pat:** it becomes an assignment from school with no plan yet; it is on Pat's link (and his Due soon, "No plan yet").
- **Hide:** it leaves his list (a parent can show it again).
- His own draft of a plan ("I'll draft it myself", §12.5) isn't built with the school feed; until it is, **Plan with Pat** is the one-tap way to plan a big one.

## 12. 0006: the school feed (`0006_homework_school.sql`, schema 6)

Its guard refuses to run without 0005 (`hub_schema_version() < 5`: "Homework (migration 0005) isn't installed"), twice ("already installed"), on a database set back to 5 ("set back to 5", §16.5), or on one that isn't as 0005 left it, as §3.1 does. It ends with `hub_schema_version()` returning 6. Its parts:
1. **Tables** (written out in full in v2-design §11):
   ```
   school_feeds (a link's settings and status; the address itself is in private.school_feed_urls)
     id, kid_id → homework_kids on delete cascade, kind ('assignments', 'schedule', 'games'), unique (kid_id, kind)
     host, url_end (4 characters: "…a1b2"), date_rule ('start' | 'end', default 'end'), date_shift (-1 to 1)
     checked (the first-import check answered), paused
     next_at, last_try_at, last_ok_at, last_count, last_repeats, last_stop (a fixed code), fails
     removed_at (Remove while items made assignments remain: the row stays only for them)
     created_*, updated_* (a read never changes them)
     RLS: members select; writes through functions.
   private.school_feed_urls (the addresses: passwords, in effect)
     feed_id PK → school_feeds on delete cascade, url
     RLS on, no policies, no privilege for anon, authenticated or service_role.
   private.school_sync (one row: the read job's lease)
     id boolean PK, run_id, lease_until, last_run_at, handed (the links the run was handed and hasn't stored, in order)
   school_items (what the links hold that the hub uses)
     id, feed_id → school_feeds on delete cascade, key (md5), has_uid, unique (feed_id, key)
     title (1 to 120), subject (the class), details (the description, at most 4,000)
     first_day, last_day, due_date (assignments); starts_at, ends_at (schedule and games: busy time)
     assignment_id → assignments on delete set null, made_at, applied (what the feed last wrote into it)
     hidden, first_seen_at, last_seen_at, missing, gone_at
     RLS: members select; writes through functions.
   assignments.added_by gains 'school'.
   ```
2. **Functions.** Parents (each 42501 "Only a parent can change homework." for anyone else): `set_school_feed(p_kid uuid, p_kind text, p_url text) returns jsonb` (Add link and Replace; null or '' is Remove: its address and every item no one made an assignment go; while items made assignments remain, the row stays for them, marked removed (`removed_at`: not read, not on the status card, the first-import check asked again), so adding the link again takes the same row and the next read finds those items by their keys instead of bringing them back as New from school; with none, the row goes too; from 0008, a removed link added again starts as a new link, not read yet: `last_try_at`, `last_ok_at`, `last_count` and `last_repeats` cleared, so nothing describes the old link's last read as the new one's, while Replace on a live link keeps them; answers `{id, kind, host, url_end}` only, or null); `check_school_feed(p_feed uuid, p_date_rule text, p_date_shift int) returns jsonb` (the first-import answer; answers the status card); `pause_school_feed(p_feed uuid, p_paused boolean) returns void` (Resume makes the link due now); `school_item_action(p_item uuid, p_action text) returns jsonb` (`'add'`, `'hide'`, `'show'`). Members: `school_feed_status(p_kid uuid) returns jsonb` (the status card; null for anyone else; replaced by 0008 with the same signature, its check filled with past items when fewer than 5 are to come, §12.8). Jordan: `school_item_by_token(p_token text, p_item uuid, p_action text, p_minutes int default null) returns jsonb` (`'quick'`, `'with_tutor'`, `'hide'`), counted. Jobs (service_role): `school_begin(p_force boolean default false) returns jsonb`, `school_store(p_run uuid, p_feed uuid, p_items jsonb, p_stop text, p_repeats int default 0) returns jsonb`, `school_finish(p_run uuid) returns void`. One more anon function: twenty in all. Their answers are in v2-design §11; the exact shapes the pages read are below (12.8).
3. **Busy time:** `private.free_minutes` (replaced: 0005's body plus marked lines) also takes his schedule and games items as busy blocks, so placement, Quick, his bars, Pat's week and `homework_week` follow.
4. **His view and Pat's** are wrapped (0005's bodies are now `private.homework_view_kid_v5` and `private.homework_view_tutor_v5`): each assignment made from school carries `school: {gone, moved_to}`, and his view carries `from_school` once he has a checked Assignments link. Without a school link both answer exactly as 0005's (the 0005 tests' exact key lists stay green). `private.homework_card` is wrapped too (0005's body is now `private.homework_card_v5`) and adds `from_school`, a count, when anything is new.
5. **`hub-jobs`:** the `school` action, `school.ts`, `ical.ts`; **no new secret**. **`cron.sql`:** `hub-school` at minute 23 of every hour (a fifth job).
6. **Not built with the school feed** (each additive, later): the homework line in `day_text` (his text and the parents' email); Jordan drafting his own plan ("I'll draft it myself", **Ready for Pat**, and the AI's "What's missing?", which is PR B's); his pace applied in placement and the bars (§5.4). Until then 0006 changes no email, text or wall event.
7. **Tests** (§14's pattern): `tests/db/school.test.sql` (the address check and its refusals; the address unreadable by anon, a parent and `service_role`, read only by `school_begin`; the lease; a read's keys, window, repeated UID and caps; an empty read, the failures and their backoff, fixed codes only; missing, gone, back again and aging out; a moved item without a UID; the first-import check; his New from school, Quick, Plan with Pat, Hide, and who may act; a parent's Add, Hide and Show; the school's changes into assignments, never over a person's; busy time in `homework_week`, Pat's week and placement; pause, homework off, Remove and Erase; row-level security; and from the review: the description emptied on the item and in `applied` 30 days after the due date by the hourly run, a paused link too, and Sam reading neither; a run that dies partway; two classes' "Homework" without UIDs; the moved-item step taking only an item the last read saw, inside the window, of the same class; Remove and Add again with no item made an assignment back as new; Resume and homework back on not stale), `tests/db/run.sh` (0006's refusals; step 5d with school links stored; step 5e, homework on and no school link: My day, his view and Pat's as 0005's), `access.test.sql` (version 6, twenty token functions, the definer lists, the private tables), `tests/fn/school.test.ts` (a realistic made-up feed, every parser case above, a 14-month Schedule of 1,500 classes of which 56 days are kept, an assignment due 200 days out, the caps, the address check, redirects to `http://`, to port 8443, with user info, to another host and a fourth, each refused, three followed; size, by Content-Length and by a body that runs on; timeouts, of the answer and of the body; each HTTP answer's code; a link's failure not stopping the others; who may ask; a fake fetch that throws `TypeError('error sending request for url (https://academy.school.example/podium/feed/iCal.aspx?z=SECRETMARKER)')`, after which SECRETMARKER is in no console line, nothing sent to the database and not the answer; and from the review: hostile bodies (200 KB of `<`, 100,000 blank lines) each read in under 500 ms of CPU; "x < 3 and y > 1" kept while real tags go; NUL, a lone surrogate, an emoji at the 4,000th and the 120th character; a fold inside an "ñ" and an emoji; `school_store` refusing a link's items), and `cron-sql.test.ts` (five jobs).
8. **The shapes the pages read** (the client builds on these):
   - Parents, Homework → Switches → School links: `school_feed_status(p_kid)` → `{kid_id, last_run_at, feeds: [{id, kind, host, url_end, checked, date_rule, date_shift, paused, last_try_at, last_ok_at, last_count, last_repeats, last_stop, fails, next_at, stale}], check: [{id, title, subject, first_day, last_day, due_date, past}], new: [{id, title, subject, due_date}], hidden: [same], moved: [{assignment_id, title, from, to}], gone: [{assignment_id, title, due_date}]}` (feeds in the order Assignments, Schedule, Games; `check` is the first-import check, empty once answered). The check (only while the Assignments link exists, isn't removed and isn't checked): on 6 and 7, its next 5 items (due today or later), so `[]` when every item is past; from 0008, up to 5 of its items still on the school site, chosen as the ones to come (`due_date` today or later), earliest first, then, when fewer than 5 are to come, the most recent past ones (`due_date` before today), latest first; the chosen ones in date order (`due_date`, `title`, `id`), each with `past` (`due_date` before today; absent before 0008). With 5 or more to come it is 6's check with `past: false` on each. The page tells its empty check apart by the Assignments link's status: not read yet (no `last_ok_at`); read with nothing in it (`last_count` 0); or read with items but all of them past (`last_count` above 0: on a database before 0008; and, until its next read, a link removed and added again on one, since 0006's `set_school_feed` keeps the old link's last read on the row; from 0008 a link added again has no `last_ok_at` until its first read). A read link's words end by its state: `paused`, resume it first; `last_stop` one of `bad_link`, `redirect`, `refused`, `gone`, `sign_in` or `not_calendar` (the stops whose words say "use Replace"), Replace it first; else the next ones show once the school posts them. The toast after **Looks right** / **Use the first date** / **One day off** is chosen from `check_school_feed`'s answer (the status card after the answer, which re-dates every item): "…shows them now." when its `new` holds anything, else "…shows the next ones once the school posts them." **Add link** and **Replace**: `set_school_feed(p_kid, p_kind, p_url)`; **Remove**: the same with `p_url` null. **Looks right** / **Use the first date** / **One day off**: `check_school_feed(p_feed, 'end' | 'start', 0 | -1 | 1)`. **Pause**: `pause_school_feed`. On an item: `school_item_action(p_item, 'add' | 'hide' | 'show')`. **Check now**: `functions.invoke('hub-jobs', {body: {action: 'school'}})` as the parent, then read the status again.
   - Jordan's `#homework`: `homework_by_token`'s `from_school: [{id, title, subject, due_date, quick}]` (absent until he has a checked Assignments link); each assignment's `school: {gone, moved_to}` when it was made from school, and `added_by: 'school'`; his card's `from_school` (a count, absent when none). Taps: `school_item_by_token(p_token, p_item, 'quick', p_minutes)`, `(…, 'with_tutor')`, `(…, 'hide')`, each answering `{assignment_id}` and his view. Its refusals: 22023 "Choose quick, with_tutor or hide.", 22023 "A step is 5 to 60 minutes.", 42501 "That isn't on your list from school.", 42501 "Plan this one with Pat.", P0001 "That one is on your homework already." (Hide on one made an assignment), 22023 "Set a homework time first.", 22023 "That can't be saved.".
   - Pat's link: each assignment's `added_by: 'school'` and `school: {gone, moved_to}`, in the list and with `p_assignment`.

## 13. Security and privacy

### 13.1 Threats, and what stops them

1. **Pat's link sees one kid's homework and nothing else.** Its four functions find the kid through `private.tutor_link_by_token` and scope every query to that kid's rows. The answers carry no family member's name (only the first word of Jordan's name and the link's own label; while the AI is on and the link may ask, Pat's preview can confirm a guessed one, §13.6), no email (`given_by`, `done_by`, `added_by` and `qa_log`'s `by` are `'kid'`, `'tutor'` or `'family'`), no events but homework time and "Busy" blocks without titles, no points, rewards or asks, no tokens. **No live status:** nothing on the link says whether he is working now, or whether he worked alone. A tutor token is never a My day token: every other token function looks up `people.day_token` only. Tested by calling every anonymous function with Pat's token. With the coach view on (§20), the link also reads that kid's points, rewards, focus sessions, his own calendar and his daily tasks, and still nothing of anyone else (§20.8).
2. **Why not a sign-in, a person or Jordan's link for Pat:** every policy is "any member", so a signed-in tutor would see the household; a `helper` gets the shopping list and appears in the parents' email blocks; Jordan's My day shows family dinners, family Google events and directory phone numbers.
3. **Free text written without a sign-in grows:** the teacher's text from Pat's link (4,000 characters), titles, questions, answers, notes, cues, suggestions, his own assignments. Each: refused over its limit before any work; cleaned (`clean_text`, or `clean_block` for the teacher's text: control, bidi and zero-width characters out); counted (Jordan 150 writes and 20 starts a day, at most 15 open assignments of his own; Pat's link 200 writes a day); labelled ("Jordan (My day)", "Pat Lee, tutor (tutor link)"); drawn only as escaped text; never in an email subject or on the wall. The teacher's text goes to the AI only after the scrub (§6.4).
4. **Leaked links.**
   - Jordan's: someone could tick his steps, add homework and run blocks (at most 20 points a day; trades need a parent).
   - Pat's: read Jordan's homework and the teacher's text and his homework hours for the coming two weeks; change his plans; add assignments and check milestones, which pays points up to the weekly milestone cap (80); and ask the AI 10 times a day (about $6 at the very worst, inside the $15 monthly limit), and, while the AI is on and the link may ask, confirm guessed names through the preview (§13.6).
   - Every write says who made it, and the parents see where a trade's points came from. **New link** (the Family page for Jordan's, the switches card for Pat's) stops it at once; Pat's link also ends by itself after 180 days, and its "last used" time shows when it was used. The README tells Pat to open the link on Pat's own phone or computer, not on Jordan's or in a shared browser.
5. **The AI:** no free prompt from the browser; who asks is checked before any lock, and the switches, the key and the caps are decided in SQL under the lock; a $15 Console limit; the parents' switch, off by default, and only after a Test and a never-send list; the names the hub knows, titled names and the never-send words taken out, and no grades, activities, links, emails, phones, tokens or ids; **What Claude will see** whenever what would be sent changed; labelled drafts; a person saves every plan; every run listed for the parents (who, when, result, estimated cost).
6. **Prompt injection** from the teacher's text or a note: named fields, and field text can't close a field (angle brackets replaced, §6.4); the rule that instructions inside them are data; no tools; structured output with short fields; links stripped; locked steps restored by the server; a person's review. The worst case is an odd plan that a person edits.
7. **Points are the database's:** server times, whole minutes seen, one open block, a daily cap on the block's own day, milestones set and checked by adults, paid once and capped by the week, every trade approved by a parent who sees where the points came from (§8.3).
8. **No labels, anywhere.** No column, word list, prompt, fixture, screenshot or doc describes a condition, and nothing asks for one. Pat's link has no field for one. A test fails if `supabase/`, `assets/`, the fixtures or the AI prompt contain a word from a list of medical and learning-label words that lives only in that test (§14).
9. **Grants:** anon has no privilege on any new table; non-members see no rows; steps, sessions, points, asks and AI runs can't be written directly by anyone; `homework_use` is reachable only by its definer function; tutor links and AI runs are readable only by the parents. The pinned lists in `access.test.sql` change on purpose, and the PR says why.
10. **Tokens are plain text and readable by the parents**, as `people.day_token` is today. No page shows Jordan a tutor token.
11. **An outside adult and a child.** Every answer and note Pat writes to Jordan is shown to the parents (Questions and notes, §9.1; Today counts open questions), can be changed by Pat only for 15 minutes, and is kept in `qa_log` when replaced or cleared.

### 13.2 Where each thing goes

| Thing | Homework page | Pat's link | Jordan's My day | AI (Anthropic) | Email, texts, wall |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Assignment title, class, due date | yes | yes | yes | yes (scrubbed) | no (0006: a title in his text and the parents' block) |
| The teacher's text | yes | yes | no | yes (scrubbed) | no |
| Steps and milestones | yes | yes | given plans | the plan being revised | no (0006: step titles in his text) |
| Questions, answers and coach's notes | yes, with earlier ones | yes | the current ones | no | no |
| Finished sessions and his notes | yes | yes ("with you" or not) | his own | no | no |
| A block running now | yes | **no** | his own | no | no |
| Alone or with a parent | yes | **no** | his own | no | no |
| Points, rewards, asks | yes | no (with the coach view on: read-only, §20) | yes | no | no |
| His pace | yes | yes | yes | yes (a number) | no |
| Busy blocks | with titles | without titles | his own events, as today | minutes only | as today |
| Names | yes | the first word of Jordan's name, the link's label | — | taken out (best effort) | as today |
| The tutor token | parents | its own | never | never | never |
| (0006) The school link | "…a1b2" | never | never | never | never |

### 13.3 How long things are kept

Done at writes (`private.homework_trim`, called by every Jordan and Pat writer, `save_homework_plan` and `homework_ai_start`), the house pattern (`list_done`, `calendar_finish`), with no new cron job:
- the teacher's text: emptied 30 days after the due date;
- AI drafts nobody used: `ai_draft` and `ai_prev` cleared 30 days after `ai_at`;
- `ai_runs`: deleted after 90 days; runs past their lease are closed (`private.ai_sweep`);
- assignments done or dropped more than 180 days ago (`done_at`, `dropped_at`; never the due date): deleted, with their steps (and so their questions, answers, notes and `qa_log`);
- focus sessions that started more than 180 days ago: deleted;
- reward asks decided more than 180 days ago: deleted;
- `homework_use`: rows older than 2 days deleted (by `count_homework`).
- (0006) the school's items: an item's description, and the copy of it in `applied`, emptied 30 days after the item's due date (as the assignment's own copy, so `school_apply` finds them equal and writes nothing back); past items nobody made an assignment deleted (assignments 14 days after the due date, schedule and games items once they end); a removed link's row once nothing made from it is left on it. These are done by every run of the school job (`private.school_trim`, from `school_begin`: every hour, and every **Check now**), whatever the link's state, so a paused link or a kid with homework off is trimmed too.

Ledger rows stay, with their links to deleted rows set null (`on delete set null`), so the balance never changes. Pace reads from the done steps that remain. Nobody sees these records after 30 days anyway (the page's "Done (30 days)", Pat's 30 days, Jordan's 3), so 180 days is room to look back over a term. Trimming waits for the next write for that kid; with no writes for months, things stay until then or until **Erase**.

### 13.4 Anthropic's terms, and minors

- **Training:** Anthropic doesn't train on API data. Inputs and outputs belong to the family.
- **Retention:** Anthropic's pages say it two ways (not kept by default; deleted within 30 days); plan for **up to 30 days**, and **up to 2 years** for anything its safety systems flag. The switches card says both. Opus 5.5 isn't one of the models that require 30-day retention (those are the Fable and Mythos models), and nor are its fallback targets, Claude Opus 5 and Opus 4.8 (§6.5).
- **Schemas** are cached for up to 24 hours; the schema holds nothing personal.
- **Minors:** Anthropic's usage policy asks organizations whose products minors use **directly** to add safeguards (AI disclosure, filtering, monitoring). Jordan never uses the AI directly; the design does the practical parts anyway (labelled drafts, structured output, short fields, no links, every run visible to the parents, a person's review). Whether a private family app counts is the owner's call (owner decision 9; not legal advice).
- **The school's own rules** on AI help are the owner's to check (owner decision 9). The planner writes no schoolwork.

### 13.5 If the hub can't be reached

The owner pastes in the Supabase SQL editor, in one go:
```sql
update public.homework_kids set active = false;
update public.household set homework_ai = false;
update public.tutor_links set active = false;
```
That stops every homework function, link and AI call, without deleting anything. Deleting the key in Anthropic's Console also stops the AI at once. The README has these lines.

### 13.6 Accepted risks

- Any parent can change any plan or check any milestone; `updated_by`, `done_by`, `checked_by` and the ledger's `who` record it.
- The end buttons are self-report; a block can be left running. Whole minutes seen, the daily cap, the parents' view of every block and the parents' approval of every trade are the controls.
- Jordan's own homework earns like any; "added by Jordan", the daily cap and a parent's **Drop** are the controls.
- The scrub is best effort (a classmate's first name can get through); the preview, open whenever the text changed, is the backstop.
- **Pat's preview confirms guessed names** (PR B: while the parents' switch is on and the link may ask). It is the message itself (§6.4), so a word typed into an assignment's text from the link that comes back as `[name]` is a name the hub knows (anyone active in the household, any tutor link's label or a word of it) or a never-send word. One write holds a few hundred guesses and the link has 200 writes a day, so a list of common first names or local schools finds the ones that match. The lists themselves can't be read, and no answer carries a name that wasn't typed in. Scrubbing Pat's preview with fewer names would make it differ from what is sent, or send names the hub knows to Anthropic. The controls: the parents' switch (off by default); the link's **Can ask the AI** (on when a parent makes the link; without it there is no preview); every write labelled "(tutor link)"; the link's "last used" time; **New link**.

## 14. Tests

All made up: the suites' own households, `example.com`, 555-01xx, a made-up school host (`academy.school.example`, with the allowed suffix replaced in tests, served by a fake fetch, never the network). `tests/run.sh` must end with `0 failed`. Everything below is PR A's unless marked **(PR B)**; the database tests of the AI's SQL side are PR A's, since 0005 holds it.

**The "Pat Lee" clash.** Pat Lee is already the made-up electrician contractor in many fixtures and assertions (`tests/db/v1_data.sql`, `tests/realdb/seed_v1.sql`, `tests/e2e/mock.js`, `tests/db/digest.test.sql`). The tutor is never a `people` row and never a contractor, so `private.short_name` and contractor lookups are unaffected; fixtures label the link "Pat Lee, tutor", and tests find it only inside the tutor-link card and Pat's view, never by the bare name.

## 15. Docs and bookkeeping in the same PR

**This document** is new and changes nothing else. The build PRs do the rest; PR B's parts are marked.
- **`docs/v2-design.md`:**
  - §0: a v1.3 line pointing here.
  - §2: the invariant names the tutor link ("…and v1.3's tutor link, whose four functions find one kid's homework by its token").
  - §3 "Anonymous token functions": the eleven new ones with their signatures, and "These eight are the only new functions anon can call" becomes **nineteen**. "Schema version": 0005 makes it 5.
  - §4: Navigation (Homework in More; a desktop tab after Food) and Pages (Homework; My day's card, `#homework`, `#focus`, `#points`, `#plan/<id>`; `day.html?h=`).
  - **(PR B)** §7 deploy step 4 and the README's "Upgrading to v2": `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optional (without it the AI action answers `not_set_up` and everything else works).
  - A new **§10 "v1.3: Homework"**: the eleven tables as `create table public.…` blocks exactly as 0005 has them, the household's three columns as its `alter table … add column` statement, every new public function's signature, every private function's name, the replaced `get_person_day`, and "0005 touches neither wrapper".
- **`README.md`:** a **Homework** section:
  - turning it on; homework time (an ordinary event, changed on the Calendar page; Private if the wall shouldn't name it); adding assignments and lessons, with the Live Text hint; starter plans; Give Jordan this plan;
  - his screen: + Homework and Quick, the timer, the stuck sheet, Re-plan;
  - **where he works:** on his phone, a scheduled Focus for homework time (iPhone: Settings → Focus → + → a "Homework" Focus → Add Schedule → the same days and times; Android: Do Not Disturb with a schedule), so other apps stay quiet; on a family computer, My day in a small window beside his work; **not on a school-managed laptop**, whose filter may log the address;
  - **set a repeating phone alarm "Homework: open My day" at his usual time**, and, optionally on an iPhone, Shortcuts → Automation → Alarm → "Homework" → Is Stopped → Run Immediately → Open URLs → his My day link with `#homework` at the end, so stopping the alarm opens his homework (check it on his phone);
  - points and rewards with made-up examples in three sizes (an instant one about 15, "15 minutes of extra game time · 15"; one about 200, "Pick the movie · 200"; one about 500, "A trip to the water park · 500"), and the review with him after a term;
  - Pat's link ("it shows only Jordan's homework; open it on your own phone or computer, not Jordan's; New link if it leaks; it ends after 180 days");
  - **(PR B)** the AI ("what is sent, and what never is"; the setup in §6.12; how to stop it);
  - what is never shown where; the three off-lines of §13.5.
- **`CLAUDE.md`** (numbered steps edited in place: the docs check's `steps()` keeps the last "4." it finds):
  - "Before every PR": the docs check also reads the function signatures in `docs/v1.3-homework.md`.
  - "Database changes" names `0005_homework.sql`; 0005 refuses to run without 0004.
  - "Client code": `index.html` loads `plan.js` after `shoplist.js` and `homework.js` after `food.js`; `day.html` loads `config.js`, supabase-js, `assets/shoplist.js`, `assets/plan.js` and `assets/day.js`, has a third mode `day.html?h=<tutor link>`, and calls only the nineteen anon token functions, named, plus (PR B) `functions.invoke('hub-jobs')` for the AI from Pat's mode.
  - "Deploy order": **(PR B)** step 4 names `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` as optional; one sentence: "For v1.3 (0005) the site goes live first, and 0004 must already be applied; it changes no Edge Function and not `cron.sql`. v1.3's AI planner changes `hub-jobs` and no migration (both orders are in `docs/v1.3-homework.md` §16)."
- **`tests/README.md`:** `homework.test.sql`, `homework.test.js`, `v4db.test.js`, the `realdb_v4t` template; (PR B) `homework-ai.test.ts`.
- **`index.html`, `day.html`:** the new tags, and one new `?v=` on every script and stylesheet in both (PR A, and again in PR B).

**The docs check (`tests/docs/docs.test.js`) must learn, in the build PRs** (not changed by this design PR; today it passes without reading this file):
- **Register this document** (PR A): add `docs/v1.3-homework.md` to the signature check (beside `docs/v1.1-google-calendar.md` and `docs/v1.2-kitchen.md`) and to the fictional-phone-number check. From then on every signature written here must have 0005's parameter names, so the migration uses the names in §3.5 (or the PR changes this document in step). A copy of the check's logic, run over this document with the signatures it declares, finds no mismatch (the earlier CHECK that called a private function with the column's name would have been one; the household's words are now checked by a trigger).
- **Anon** (PR A): `ANON` gains the eleven new functions; the number map, which stops at "twelve", goes to "twenty", so "These nineteen are the only new functions anon can call" (and 0006's twenty) can be counted.
- **The new secret** (PR B): `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` joins the list of secrets `hub-jobs` reads and the `OPTIONAL` set; deploy step 4 in CLAUDE.md, v2-design §7 and the README names it.
- **Tables written out:** `FULL_FROM` stays `'0004'`; every table, column and constraint 0005 adds must appear in v2-design §10's sql blocks as 0005 has it.
- **Optional:** a count check for this document's §2.1 ("twenty-five new public functions … and twenty-six new private ones"), as 0004's.
- **The label-word check** (§13.1 item 8) is a new check, in the docs suite or beside it, over `supabase/`, `assets/`, the test fixtures and (PR B) the AI prompt; its word list lives only in that test.

## 16. Deploy order and the owner's steps

### 16.1 PR A: v1.3 "Homework" (0005)

No Edge Function changes, so v1.2's order applies: the site first, then the migration.
1. `tests/run.sh` ends with `0 failed`. Merge the PR. The site goes live on schema 3 or 4 and nothing changes for the family: no Homework tab, no new request, My day as before (`v3db.test.js` and `v4db.test.js` prove it).
2. **Owner, about 5 minutes, only if v1.2 isn't applied yet:** paste `supabase/migrations/0004_meals_and_shopping.sql` into the Supabase SQL editor → **Run** (docs/v1.2-kitchen.md §15). The Food tab appears after a reload.
3. **Owner, about 5 minutes:** paste `supabase/migrations/0005_homework.sql` → **Run**. "isn't installed" means 0004 is missing; "already installed" means it's done; either way nothing was changed. Reload the hub: **More → Homework**. Never before step 1 is live.
4. **No `hub-jobs` deploy and no `cron.sql` run** for PR A.
5. **Owner, about 10 minutes, on a computer:** Homework → **Turn on homework for Jordan** → homework time (days, from, to) → **Add** (a second time for Sunday if it differs) → three rewards (about 15 and instant, 200 and 500 points; the first is ticked **Instant**) → check the points values and **Focus length offered first** (10) on the switches card → **Pat Lee, tutor**: label, language → **WhatsApp** or **Text**.
6. **Owner with Jordan, about 10 minutes (the try-out):** add his real next assignment and paste the teacher's text → a starter plan → change a step → **Give Jordan this plan**. On Jordan's phone: My day shows the card; he types his start cue and if-stuck line; he taps **Start** on the card, watches the points count up, lets the ring run out and sees his points arrive, taps **Step done**; he sees the next-reward bar, and how **Get it now** works on the instant reward; he adds tonight's homework himself with **+ Homework** and **Quick**. Set the Homework Focus on his phone (2 minutes) and the repeating alarm "Homework: open My day".
7. **Tell Pat** (one message): the link shows only Jordan's homework; open it on your own phone or computer; plan at the next session; keep the link private.

Owner's time: **about 25 minutes** (steps 3, 5 and 6), plus 5 if step 2 is needed, plus the 3 minutes of §1.3 now.

### 16.2 PR B: v1.3 "AI planner" (no migration)

`hub-jobs` changes, so v1.1's order applies to it: the site and the function, and nothing to paste. It goes after PR A and 0005.
1. `tests/run.sh` ends with `0 failed`. Merge the PR. The AI parts appear on the Homework page and Pat's link, with the AI off.
2. **Claude deploys `hub-jobs`** from main with `verify_jwt` off (`supabase/config.toml` pins it for the CLI; with the dashboard or the Supabase MCP's `deploy_edge_function`, turn it off by hand). Smoke checks: a POST with no header still answers 401 `{"error":"Not allowed."}`; `{action: 'homework_ai', kind: 'test', key: '<a new uuid>'}` with an admin's sign-in answers 200 `{"stop": "not_set_up"}` (the key isn't set yet). The morning email and the calendar job are unchanged.
3. **Optional, owner, about 15 minutes, on a computer:** the AI setup (§6.12): Anthropic account and $15 limit, the key into Supabase, words never to send, **Test** answers "Working", then turn the AI planner on.
4. At the next session Pat can **Send to Claude**.

### 16.3 Undoing v1.3

- **Homework off** for Jordan on the switches card: his card goes and Pat's link answers null at once. **AI planner off** stops every AI request. Nothing is deleted.
- To hide v1.3 altogether, turn it off **first** (the switches live on the Homework page, which disappears with version 5). The owner pastes, in one go:
  ```sql
  update public.homework_kids set active = false;
  update public.household set homework_ai = false;
  create or replace function public.hub_schema_version() returns int language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$ select 4 $$;
  ```
  (the same definition 0004 has, with 4). The Homework tab and the Today lines go; Jordan's My day is as on 4; the homework-time events stay on the calendar (delete them there if wanted). `hub-jobs` answers the AI action with `off`. Running 0005 again says "set back to 4"; switching back is the same `create or replace` with 5, then **Homework on**.
- 0005 is additive; every other page works the same on 4 and 5. PR B can be reverted on its own (the AI parts go; nothing in the database changes).

### 16.4 0006

`hub-jobs` and `cron.sql` change, so v1.1's order applies: **the site and `hub-jobs` before 0006**.
1. `tests/run.sh` `0 failed`; merge (the school parts appear only on schema 6). The site goes live.
2. **Claude deploys `hub-jobs`** from main with `verify_jwt` off (`supabase/config.toml` pins it for the CLI; with the dashboard or the Supabase MCP's `deploy_edge_function`, turn it off by hand). **No new secret.** The `school` action answers 503 `no_schema` until step 3. Smoke check: a POST of `{"action": "school"}` with no header answers 401 `{"error":"Not allowed."}`; the morning email and the calendar job are unchanged.
3. **Owner, 5 minutes:** paste `0006_homework_school.sql` → **Run**. "isn't installed" means 0005 is missing; "already installed" means it's done; either way nothing was changed. Never before steps 1 and 2 are live.
4. **Owner, 2 minutes:** paste `supabase/cron.sql` → **Run** (it adds `hub-school`, every hour at 23 minutes past; safe to run again).
5. **Owner, about 5 to 10 minutes, on a computer:** the Blackbaud links on a parent's login (§11.2), **Check now**, and the first-import check.

Owner's time: **about 15 minutes.**

### 16.5 Undoing 0006

- **Remove** a link on the switches card: its address and every item not made an assignment go at once; assignments made from it stay (and the items they were made from, off the card, so adding the link again doesn't show them as new). **Pause** stops reading one and keeps its items.
- To stop every read: `select cron.unschedule('hub-school');` (and `cron.sql` again to bring it back).
- To hide the school parts altogether, paste `create or replace function public.hub_schema_version() returns int language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$ select 5 $$;` (the same definition 0005 has, with 5). The pages go back to schema 5's; his My day keeps New from school until his Assignments link is removed (its rows stay). Running 0006 again says "set back to 5"; switching back is the same `create or replace` with 6.

### 16.6 0008

No Edge Function and no `cron.sql` change, so v1.2's order applies: **the site that understands it first, then 0008**, after 0007.
1. `tests/run.sh` ends with `0 failed`. Merge. The site goes live on schema 7 and works there as before: an empty check now says why (not read yet; read, with nothing in it; read, every item past; paused, or needing Replace), and the page has no version gate.
2. **Owner, about 1 minute:** paste `supabase/migrations/0008_school_check.sql` → **Run**. "isn't installed" means 0007 is missing; "already installed" means it's done; "isn't as migration 0007 left it" means something it replaces or reads is missing; either way nothing was changed. Never before step 1 is live.
3. On the next load an Assignments link not yet checked shows its latest past items when nothing is due yet ("was due …"): **Looks right**, **Use the first date** or **One day off**, as before.
